### Roger's nineteenth pass (2026-11-12)

1. **Is the README a good place for the pets' everyday looks?** Answered in the conversation: yes (a short GIF at the top, a strip of all seven, a few moments); not built until Roger picks how (real Windows recordings or clean renders).
2. **One go for every pet's size** (「希望加入一鍵式調整寵物大小」), built: 大家的大小 with four choices, 小 70%, 中 100%, 大 130%, 特大 160% (`chonkgene_platform::tray::SIZES`, the one list), in three places: the pet's menu (「大家的大小…」, a page with the current one marked 「目前」), the tray (a submenu), and settings 一般 (a row of four buttons, the current one filled; none when the pets differ). `Msg::SizeAll`, `App::size_all`: every pet takes the size; those out on the desktop change where they stand, facing the same way, with a little hop, and without coming in and saying hello again (a single pet's size change still comes in afresh); resting ones keep it for when they come out. An open settings window takes sizes changed elsewhere unless an edit of its own is on its way. The Windows tray code was checked with clippy for the Windows target (stub C compiler); not run there.

### Roger's twentieth pass (feedback only, 2026-11-12; to do next)

Suggested order (agreed 2026-11-13): 4 (the run's 同手同腳, a bug with its cause known; built 2026-11-13) → 2 (see-through child hands that press in; built 2026-11-13) → 3 (scenes from the menu) → 5 (the beach) → 1 (the fox's lag: (a) frame-fair budget first, then (b) a lighter tail mesh; (c) a bigger budget only if Roger wants it after).

1. **The fox, with all she does, still lags: not enough frames.** First suspects: her mesh is by far the heaviest (nine tails, most of her skin is tail), so each frame costs more GPU time and the fair-share budget (`gpu.rs` `Budget`) gives her fewer; her skinning on the CPU (nine tail chains) per frame; and her extra layers (fox-fire, glow, the tails' fan in gestures and hugs). Plan: read her `frames:` lines in `chonkgene.log` from Roger's Windows machine (shown vs asked per second, GPU ms a frame, held back), measure her CPU pose time here against the Shiba's, then pick: a cheaper tail mesh (fewer rings), per-species cost in the budget, or a floor on her frame rate while she moves.

   **Found in Roger's log (2026-11-13, `inbox/chonkgene.log`, 16.6 h on the UHD 770, seven pets, 25% budget)**: the GPU is not slow, the budget is shared unfairly for her. Every 10 s the seven together use the whole budget (GPU time 25.0–27.1%). Each pet's frames cost 1.3–1.7 ms of GPU time; hers cost 2.2–2.8 ms (her nine tails: about twice as much skin). `Budget` gives each pet the same share of GPU *time*, so she gets about half the frames of the others: at 60 s she showed 15.4 of 44.1 asked a second (287 held back), the others 21–27; at 200 s 16.7 of 48.1 (313 held back) against 21–27. She also asks for the most (lively, tails always moving), so the gap shows most on her. Fixes, in order: (a) the fair share counted in frames, not time: weight each pet's share by its cost a frame, so a costly one gets the time for as many frames as the rest; (b) a lighter tail mesh for her (fewer rings along each tail, the tips merged) to bring 2.8 ms near 1.7; (c) only then think about the budget itself (seven pets now by default: 25% is spent in full, everyone gets 21–27 of 34–45 frames asked; 30–35% on a UHD 770 is still small, Roger to decide).
2. **The hug's hands are still odd, and so is how they touch** (「手臂不用出現，只要手指和手腕，並改為透明，可以考慮小孩的手。手要碰到寵物身上，並有觸摸物理互動」). Plan, proposed to Roger the same day: no arms, only a child's hands from the wrist (fingers spread a little), see-through (about 60–70% opaque, soft edges, so the fur shows through); placed on the pet's own outline at its sides each frame (not fixed to the bounds at the start), so they rest on the body; and they press into it: the mesh dents under each hand (the finger dent, `mesh_gpu` `press`, takes one point now: two needed, so a shader change), the body squashes in a little between them and bulges, then springs back; the fingers stroke a little while they hold it.

   **Built (2026-11-13)**: the arms are gone (`baby_arm`, `hug_arms` removed). `props::hug_hands` places a child's hand on each side of the body every frame, at the belly (環抱) or under the front legs or wings (前抱, the fingers pointing up more), from `MeshRenderer::body_at` and `body_edge` (the outermost vertex of belly, back, bottom and legs on that row: never the tails or wings, so the fox's tails and the birds' spread wings do not catch the hands); `draw_hug_hands` draws them (plump palm, four fingers a little apart, a thumb on top, rosy tips, a soft halo, the wrist rubbed out) into a layer put down at 66%, so the fur shows through and overlaps are no darker; the fingers curl in turn and each hand slides up and down a little (stroking). They press in: the shader takes two presses now, each with its own direction (`mesh_gpu::Press`; a finger pushes into the screen, a hand in from the side), centred on the outline vertex itself (the vertex nearest the camera there would be on its front and barely dent the outline); the body squeezes narrower and taller between them (`HAND_SQUEEZE`), in slow pulses, and springs back when they let go. The Shiba's belly row goes from 76 to 69 px held (`tests/mesh.rs` `hug_hands_press_in_on_its_sides_and_let_go`). `CHONKGENE_STAGE=hug` hugs the first pet (checked in the app under Xvfb with dumped frames). Review GIF `inbox/child-hands.gif`; README `docs/media/hugs.gif` redone. Owed: Roger's look on Windows (the hands on the white fox are pale; a warmer outline if he finds them hard to see).
3. **Scenes called up from the right-click menu too** (「接下來場景也可以用右鍵呼喚出來」). Now a pet's little scenes come by themselves (`maybe_scene`), except the dance (叫牠… › 跳舞, 放聚光燈) and the fox's fire (施法… › 狐火). Plan: a page 「場景…」 in the pet's menu listing its own scenes (the duckling's pond with its beach, the cats' sunbeam and butterfly, the Shiba's bone, the chick's seeds, the owl's branch, the fox's fire) and, for anyone, the shared ones (a pond and beach to get into, a sunbeam to lie in); a scene already out is offered as 「收起來」; asleep or busy, the page says so instead of doing nothing; and the same in the tray (for whichever pet is nearest the middle, or one picked).

   **Built (2026-11-13)**: a page 「場景…」 on every pet's menu (asleep too), listing its own scenes first (`Species::scenes`), then, after a line, the shared ones it has not got (`Scene::SHARED`: 玩水池, 曬太陽; `Species::menu_scenes`). Rows: 玩水池, 曬太陽, 追蝴蝶, 啃骨頭, 啄種子, 飛上樹枝, 狐火, each with an icon (new: `Icon::Scenery`, `Pond`, `Butterfly`, `Seeds`, `Branch`; the bone uses the snack's bone). In a scene already, the page offers only to end it: 「把水池收起來」 (and so on), 「從水池上岸」 for one that joined someone's pond, 「不跳了」 in the spotlight put down from the menu; it ends as it would at its time (out of the water with a shake, the butterfly off, back down from the branch; `play::wind_down`), or, not yet at its thing, the thing is simply taken away. Asleep, the page says 「牠睡著了，醒來再玩」; busy (held, in a skit, chasing, doing as asked, …), 「牠在忙，等等再叫牠」; up on a window, the scenes whose thing lies on the floor (`Scene::on_floor`) show 「在高處」 and do nothing (`play::not_now`). Asked from the menu, its own next scene is put off as after one of its own (`play::call_scene`). Pets in a pond or a sunbeam that is not their own say new lines (`scene_water`, `scene_sun`, in each species' voice). The tray has a submenu 「場景」 with the same seven: it puts the scene away if a pet is in it, else asks the free pet that can, nearest the middle of the screen (`tray_pick`). Found on the way: a pet leaving the pond kept throwing water in the water fight and stopped each time, so getting out took up to 16 s; now one on its way out is left out of it (5–7 s under Xvfb). Seen: `CHONKGENE_STAGE=tray-pond` asks the tray for a pond, then again 8 s later (put away); under Xvfb the Shiba got in, the Fold joined, the Shiba came out and the pond went. The menu pages rendered headless looked right; Roger's look on Windows is owed.
4. **Running looks 同手同腳 (both legs of a side together).** Not meant: a bug of the eighteenth pass's run. The walk is a lateral sequence (phases: hind left 0, front left 0.25, hind right 0.5, front right 0.75); the run was to be a bound (front pair 0 and 0.07, hind pair 0.5 and 0.57), but `skin.rs` blends the two by moving each leg's *phase* linearly (`ph + offset + run * (bound - offset)`), and half way the left legs land at 0.25 and 0.125, the right at 0.535 and 0.41: a pace, same-side legs together, every time a run speeds up or slows down (`run` follows the walking speed `s`, which ramps). Plan: blend the joint angles of the two gaits, not their phases (the walk's legs and the gallop's computed each from its own phases, mixed by `run`); and run as a gallop rather than a bound (hind left, hind right a beat apart, then front left, front right, as a dog or a cat runs), checked frame by frame in a side-on render (`examples/touch.rs` gets a `run` scene).

   **Built (2026-11-13)**: worse than thought: runs are ordered at pace 1.5–2.2 and the bound only came in from 1.7 to 2.5, so most runs sat in the middle of the mix the whole way, not only while speeding up (pace 1.9: the left legs' swing correlated 0.62). Blending joint angles instead would have the same trouble (a mix held half way swings each leg at half size), so the run is a gallop placed against the walk so that each leg's phase moves at most a third of a step (`GALLOP`, `leg_phase`): through every mix the two legs of a side stay a quarter to half a step apart, never together, and the in-between reads as a trot. The gallop comes in between pace 1.3 and 2.0 (`RUN_PACES`), the feet down for less of each step (`RUN_DUTY` 0.45, so all four leave the floor) and lifted higher; the bounce and the back's flex keep time with the front paws. Tests: the phases at every mix, and each species' real `Walk` at nine paces (same-side swing correlation under 0.4; the old code fails it). `examples/touch.rs` has `jog` (1.6) and `run` (2.2) scenes and `SLOW=n`. Review GIF `inbox/run-gait.gif` (half speed). Owed: Roger's look on Windows.
5. **A bigger beach, with ups and downs, and more to do on it** (Roger, 2026-11-13: 「水池黃金沙灘可以再做大一點，有高低起伏。並與動物有更多互動，如狗會挖沙把自己埋起來只留給頭並對你吐舌頭傻笑，在水中有時會對其它同伴噴水，或突然游泳暴走…等」). Plan:
   - **Bigger, with dunes**: wider than 1180, a low ridge or two drawn in the sand (light on the side toward the sky, shade behind). The terrain is flat ledges, so pets will not really climb: a pet on a dune is lifted to follow its outline (as `Fun::pond_lift` lifts one in the water), which reads as walking over it.
   - **The dog buries itself**: it digs (`Act::Dig`, sand flying), lies down in the hole, and a mound of sand is drawn over its body in its own window (as the pond's water is, `compose`), only the head out; it looks at you with its tongue out, grinning (`Look::Pant`), for a while, then bursts out and shakes the sand off. The Shiba first; the fox perhaps, the cats not (they would rather not).
   - **Spraying the others in the water**: a water fight is already built (`water_fight`, sixteenth pass); make it come oftener and show more, and add a spout from the duckling's bill or a dog's mouth at whoever is nearest.
   - **Swimming zoomies**: now and then one in the pond suddenly paddles round it at full speed, a wake and splashes behind it, then stops as if nothing happened.
   - Ideas to offer Roger with it: shaking off water over whoever is near when it gets out, a dog rolling in the sand after a swim (and coming out sandy), footprints that fade, a little sandcastle the chick pecks down.

   **Built (2026-11-16)**:
   - **Bigger, with dunes**: the beach is 1600 × 170 (was 1180 × 150), and its sand now runs a little past the pets' feet toward you (`BEACH_BELOW`; before, they walked in front of its fading edge). Two dunes beside the pond (`play::DUNES`, `dune_height`: each a gentle slope up the side the wind comes from, a steeper one down, both kept inside the full sand, no steeper than 0.6 px a pixel). Drawn as mounds rising from the line the pets walk along, 1.7 times as high as their feet go (`DUNE_TALL`: they walk up the near slope, not along the top), lit on the slope toward the sun (up to the right, as the sunbeam), in shade on the other, the light and shade parting at the crest as on real dunes, wind ripples, a shadow cast on the sand behind; their foot melts into the flat. A first try worked the sand out as a height field row by row, and with the desktop's low view it read as a pale sheet, not a dune. A pet on the sand rises with it (`Playground::dune_under`, eased, `Fun::dune_lift`, scaled by how far the beach has faded in). The beach is drawn once in full and only copied fainter while it fades (`PropWin::still`).
   - **The Shiba buries itself** (`bury`): standing free on the sand, at 5% a second (not again for 75 s), it digs (2.4 s, sand flying), lies down, and a heap of sand is piled over it in its own window (`props::mound_for`, `sand_mound`): as wide as all of it, over every part of its body beside and behind its head but no higher than a little under the middle of the head, which stays clear inside an oval found round its nose (lying, it faces you; the top of the head, a point on the skull, shows off to one side), with darker sand banked under the chin; the curled tail pokes out. It grins at you with its tongue out (`PoseParams::grin`, `Look::Pant`) and says so (`Line::Buried`, the Shiba's only), then after 7–12 s, or touched, picked up, asleep, hugged, the beach gone, it bursts out: the heap sinks, clumps fly off its top each way, and it shakes. The fox and the cats do not (as planned).
   - **More water fight**: a throw every 0.9–2.0 s (was 1.3–2.7); the one hit gets drops bursting off its head and running down (`Fx::Soaked`); the duckling and the Shiba spit a jet from the bill or the mouth half the time (`Spray::spout`, a thinner, flatter stream from the mouth to the other's body).
   - **Swimming zoomies** (`swim_zoomies`): on about half its visits to a pond, 5–15 s in, a pet suddenly paddles end to end of it at pace 2.4 for 3–5 s, splashing all the way, a wake of foam behind it in a V (`props::pond_wakes`), then goes back to paddling about as if nothing happened. Out of the water fight while it is at it.
   - `CHONKGENE_STAGE=beach`: the duckling's pond with the Shiba put on a dune; it buries itself at once and zoomies come soon. Seen under Xvfb (dumped windows put together): the Shiba dug in, was buried and burst out 12 s later; spits, throws and three zoomies in 40 s. `examples/beach.rs` draws the beach with the line the feet follow; `examples/touch.rs` has a `bury` scene. Review GIF `inbox/beach-play.gif` (3–19 s of that run). Found on the way: `examples/props.rs` panicked (wider things than its cells overflowed); fixed.
   - Owed: Roger's look on Windows; the ideas above still to offer him.

### Roger's twenty-first pass (feedback only, 2026-11-13; to do next)

Order (Roger, 2026-11-13: clear what is planned first): 3 (the pond's stacking, a small bug with its cause known) → 1 and 2 together (how hands really hold, and the cradle) → then the twentieth pass's 3 (scenes from the menu), 5 (the beach) and 1 (the fox's lag) as agreed. Item 4 waits for the RPG work.

1. **Hands that hold as real hands do** (a revision of the twentieth pass's item 2, built the same day; Roger: 「前抱在物理上的考量是不是應該姆指在內四指在外」「不同的生物適合的前抱和環抱的位置是不是應該不一樣」「有些動物手要在前面」). Now both holds are two palms pressed on its sides. Plan:
   - **前抱 (lifted, you in front of it)**: thumbs on its chest, toward you; the four fingers round its ribs to its back, hidden behind it, only their tips peeking past the outline. So a hand is drawn in two layers: the part behind the pet into its window *under* its picture (hidden where the body is), the part in front over it. The thumbs dent into the screen; the fingers behind push it forward (a slight bulge toward you, not a dent).
   - **環抱 (wrapped round from behind, as on your lap facing out)**: the hands in front, crossed or one over the other on its belly or chest, the wrists going round its sides to behind it and out of sight. Dents into the screen under them.
   - **Where, by species** (a table on `Species`, as `fondness` is):

     | | 環抱 (hands in front) | 前抱 (lifted) |
     |---|---|---|
     | Shiba, fox | crossed under the chest, behind the front legs | under the front legs, thumbs on the chest, fingers behind; the hind legs dangle |
     | Cats (Munchkin, Fold) | crossed on the belly (lower on the short-legged Munchkin) | one hand under the chest (thumb in front), the other under its bottom: the way vets say to lift a cat, which dislikes hanging from its armpits |
     | Chick, duckling, owl | over its folded wings, fingers across the breast (holding a bird keeps its wings in) | cupped from below like a bowl, palms up under its belly, fingers up its sides, all in front |
   - New hand shapes for these: the back of a hand (crossed in front), palms up (cupping), fingers bent round out of sight. `body_at` / `body_edge` already give the rows and sides; the shader's two directional presses already take a dent into the screen.
   **Built (2026-11-13, with item 2)**: `HugHold` (Wrap 環抱, Carry 舉高高, Cradle 搖搖抱) moved to the core, and `Hug::ByYou(HugHold)` carries it to the poses. `props::hug_hands` follows the table above (`Build::of` the species: dog, fox, cat with the Munchkin lower, bird) with three grips (`Grip`): `Thumb` (the palm and fingers drawn *behind* its picture, so only the wrist shows past its outline, the thumb over its chest), `Back` (the back of a hand: knuckle dimples, little nails, fingers together; crossed on a dog's chest or a cat's belly, over a bird's wings, not crossed), `Cup` (palm up under it, fingers up its front). `draw_hug_hands` takes a `HandLayer` (Behind before its picture, Front after), each hand on its own sheet so one's wrist fade does not rub out the other. Presses: thumbs and backs into the screen at the vertex nearest you, cups up from below at the bottom of its body (`MeshRenderer::body_bottom`, legs left out, so a bird is cupped under its belly, not its feet); the squeeze as before. Not done: the fingers behind pushing it forward (the shader has two presses, both used by the hands in front). Held, its pose changes by hold: front paws resting (環抱) or up and hind legs dangling (舉高高); a bird's wings stay folded in your hands. Test `hands_hold_each_body_its_own_way` (an oval body, every build and hold).
   **Revised (2026-11-13, Roger: 「通常狗貓的舉高高是不是都是捧著前腳腋下？」, then 「根據你的建議做就好」)**: 舉高高 is under the front legs' armpits for dogs and cats alike (the everyday lift; vets would add a hand under a cat's bottom, but that is not what 舉高高 looks like, and 環抱 already holds it from below). The thumbs go on the armpits' row (`MeshRenderer::armpits`: between the tops of `leg_fl` and `leg_fr`), and lifted it no longer sits: its body upright, front legs pushed up by your hands, hind legs hanging straight down and swinging; a cat hangs long (more upright, its hips sagging away from its chest); raised in its picture so the hanging legs stay in it (the fox more). README `docs/media/hugs.gif`: the last two cells are now 舉高高 (the Fold, hanging long) and 搖搖抱 (the Shiba, rocked, eyes closing).
2. **A third hold: 搖搖抱, cradled like a baby** (Roger: 「是不是要再加一個像嬰兒抱的臂抱」). On its back, belly up, one hand under its head and shoulders, the other under its bottom (hands only, no arms, as Roger asked in the twentieth pass), rocked gently side to side; its eyes close by degrees and, rocked long enough, it falls asleep (the sleep of §1.12). Dogs and cats lie limp, paws soft. The fox cannot lie on her back for her tails (as now when laughing: she sits up), so she sits sideways in your hands, tails draped over them, 傲嬌: she says she does not want this, then melts. Birds are never laid on their backs (a chicken turned over freezes, not cute): for them it is cupping, as in 前抱.
   - **Triggers, no new keys**: 抱抱 in the pet's menu becomes a page, 環抱 / 舉高高 / 搖搖抱, to pick one; a double-click picks by how it is: sleepy, late or tired from play → 搖搖抱; very pleased or full of energy → 舉高高; otherwise 環抱; weighted by the species (cats lifted less, most cats do not like it). So a double-click differs from time to time, for a reason one can see.
   **Built (2026-11-13)**: 搖搖抱 lasts `CRADLE_SECS` 9 s (`Hug::secs`), lifted 14 px; dogs and cats roll onto their backs (the laugh's roll-over, `Walk::cradle`), limp, paws half folded and swinging, rocked 11° at 0.45 Hz; the fox sits up, rocked, her tails hanging down over your hands; birds are cupped. Its eyes close by degrees (`cradle_eyes`: open 1.5 s, shut by 70%), never blinking open, and at the end it falls asleep (`Pet::cradle_step`, `Mind::put_to_sleep`). New lines `cradled` (at the start: the fox 「哼！才不要這樣抱！」) and `cradle_melt` (at 40%: drowsy murmurs, the fox 「……一下下就好喔。」). Menu: 「抱抱…」 opens the page 環抱 / 舉高高 / 搖搖抱 (asleep it stays 「抱抱」, it only stirs); a double-click picks with `HugHold::pick` (sleepy hours or late → cradle, pleased or lively → lifted, cats lifted a fifth as often), test `a_double_click_holds_it_by_how_it_is`. Held, it no longer wanders off (in the app a 搖搖抱 was cut short by a run half way) nor watches the cursor, and if a hug is cut short the hands leave with it. `CHONKGENE_STAGE=wrap|carry|cradle` holds the first pet so; checked in the app under Xvfb with the mesh renderer (`CHONKGENE_RENDERER=gpu`): the fox cradled, eyes closing, asleep at 8.9 s. `examples/touch.rs` scenes `hug-you`, `hug-carry`, `hug-cradle`. Review GIF `inbox/holds.gif` (Shiba, Fold, owl, fox × the three holds). Owed: Roger's look on Windows; the cups at a bird's feet are small, and the fox's tails drape by a guessed angle.
3. **In the pond, the pet further back covers the one in front** (Roger: 「在水池中，在上方寵物身體會壓過下方寵物的身體」). Checked: the pets' windows are only ever raised in the order of the pets' list (`play.rs` raises every pet after a new thing comes out, `pets.iter()`), never by depth; in a pond each pet stands at a depth of its own (`pond_deep` 0.24–0.40 of the pond up from its near edge, its window lifted by `Fun::pond_lift`), so whichever was raised last is on top whether it is in front or not, and its body (with the pond's front drawn in its window) covers the nearer one. Fix: restack by depth, the furthest first and the nearest last, whenever someone's depth changes (into or out of a pond, a depth picked) as well as on every restack; a pet you hold, and the one in front in a pet-pet hug, stay on top as now. A test on the order, and a look in the app (dumped frames, `CHONKGENE_STAGE=pond`).
   **Built (2026-11-13)**: every go round the pets' windows are put in order, bottom first (`play::stack_order`): those on top (held, hugged by you, the one in front in a pet-pet hug, so the hug no longer raises it by itself) after all the rest, the others the furthest back first, by `Fun::pond_depth` (where it is going in the pond, not where the water has eased it to, so the order does not flicker as it wades in; 0 out of the water, so those on the bank are nearest); otherwise as listed. They are raised again only when that order changes (or a thing came out), so Windows is not asked to repaint for nothing; the bubbles go over every pet, a butterfly over it all. Test `windows_stack_from_the_back_of_the_pond_forward`. In the app under Xvfb (`CHONKGENE_STAGE=pond`, X's stacking order read once a second): with five in the pond the windows went from the highest (furthest back) to the lowest, the bank after, the whole time. Before and after, put together from dumped frames: `inbox/pond-stack.png` (before: the owl at the back covered the fox in front of it). Owed: Roger's look on Windows.
4. **Keyboard shortcuts, for later** (deferred, Roger 2026-11-13: considered only when the RPG work formally starts; until then the plan's open items come first. Roger: 「鍵盤快捷還是可以規劃，接下來我有可能會加入rpg要素如打寶、探險、升級、裝備、戰鬥…等」). Not for the hugs (above), but the RPG side will want keys. Design notes to start from: the pets' windows never take the keyboard (so as not to get in the way of typing, §1.7), so keys work in two ways only: (a) a global hotkey, as Ctrl+Alt+P already is (`chonkgene_platform::hotkey`, §1.10), kept to one or two (opening the adventure window, perhaps a quick action), each changeable in settings and checked for clashes; (b) inside a window that has the keyboard because you opened it (an adventure / inventory window, like the settings window), where the rest live (moving, picking up, using, the bag, skills), shown on screen and changeable. On Wayland global keys are limited (§4): there, (b) and the menu do everything. The RPG elements themselves go to §9 to be planned with Roger.

5. **Asleep on its back, the belly's rise is too pointed** (Roger, 2026-11-13: 「仰睡的肚子起伏太尖了，不夠圓滾滾」). Cause: the breath pushed the belly's soft-spot bone out along itself (0.24); its skin follows with full weight only within a third of the spot's radius, so a narrow plateau rose higher than it was wide, a ridge. **Built (2026-11-13)**: a joint can grow (`Joint::swell`, uniform scale about its pivot), and on its back the belly grows with each breath (`BELLY_UP_SWELL` 0.4 at the top of a breath) about a middle `BELLY_SWELL_DEPTH` 0.3 deeper in the body than the spot's own (about that, just under the skin, it would bulge sideways more than up); the shift is now only the wobble and the small breath of other poses. Rounder dome, the rise as big as before (Shiba 10 px, Munchkin 17 px at 100%). Test `a_belly_up_sleeper_swells_round_with_each_breath`. Before and after, zoomed: `inbox/belly-round.gif`. Roger, the same day (柴犬): 「起伏有點太大了，可以再小一點」: `BELLY_UP_SWELL` 0.4 → 0.25, the Shiba's belly top now rises about 12 px a breath instead of 16 (the Munchkin's 21 instead of 24; the whole body's 3–4 px unchanged), still a dome; `inbox/belly-round.gif` redone (before = 0.4).

#### From Roger's second log (2026-11-13, `inbox/chonkgene.log`, 40 min on the UHD 770, seven pets)

- **Double-click hugs that came to nothing**: 16 of 49 hugs were refused, logged only as 「not now」 (the pet said its hugged line and looked happy, but no hands came). Eight in a row were on the chick at 423–428 s: standing, awake, not in a skit, preening: the habit of a pet lounging while you read (`curious`, `Activity::Reading`, `lounge_until` 20–40 s). `hug_you` refused whenever `Fun::busy`, which counts that leisure (and sitting by you, `hold_until`, and coming to you, `goto`) as busy: right for its own wandering, wrong for your hug. **Fixed**: a hug now waits only for what the pet is really up to (`Fun::engaged`: chasing, an order, a scene, catnip, a swat, carrying, hiding) and ends its leisure (`Fun::end_leisure`); a refused hug logs why (asleep, in a mood, in a skit, being dragged, not on its feet, busy). The Munchkin's four refusals at 438–446 s (walking) had no reason in the log; the next log will say.
- **A negative GPU time** (「GPU -1236.8 ms a frame」, 「GPU time -27.4%」 at 31 s): a mesh baked again (a new size or coat) gave a canvas counting from zero while the report still subtracted the old one's count. **Fixed** (`MeshFrames::install` carries the old canvas's unreported frames into the stats and starts counting afresh). Only the log and the budget's view of it were wrong, not the pictures.
- **The fox still lags** (the twentieth pass's item 1, still to do): 17.4 of 44.7 frames a second asked (39%), the others 52–62%; her frames 2.8 ms of GPU, theirs 1.4–1.7 ms. The seven together use 27% of GPU time on average (budget 25%, at most 39%), so every pet gets only about 60% of the frames it asks for, and she, costing twice as much, about 40%. The fix planned there stands: (a) a frame-fair budget first, (b) a lighter tail mesh.

#### Activities true to each animal (standing list)

Roger, 2026-11-13: 「只要做出儘可能做出符合寵物特性的活動，你可以自由新增到 plan」. Activities that real animals of each kind do and the pets do not yet, added here as they come to mind (none built; each goes to Roger's table of what next when its turn comes). What they already do (`Species::habits`): looking about, stretching, yawning, waving, digging and scratching (dogs, fox), washing (cats, fox), shaking, pecking and preening (birds), kneading, the scenes of §8.

- **Shiba**: the 柴犬 "Shiba scream" when something is not to its liking (a quick protest, dramatic, then fine); refusing to walk on (planting all four feet, 柴犬拒走) when a walk is not its idea; chasing its own tail in circles; the zoomies after a bath; carrying a toy about to show it off; sleeping with its legs straight up in the air; sneezing when excited (a play sneeze).
- **Cats (Munchkin, Fold)**: knocking a small thing off a window's top edge, looking at you while doing it; loafing (the "bread loaf", paws tucked in); slow blinks at you (built) answered by a head bonk on the cursor; sitting in a box that appears; making biscuits on a soft spot; the Munchkin sitting up on its haunches like a meerkat (Munchkins are known for it); the Munchkin's slow walk with a touch of a pace (same-side legs nearly together, as cats walk slowly anyway, and cute on its short legs; only at a stroll, never running: see item 4); the Fold sitting in the "Buddha" pose, legs out, belly up (Scottish Folds are known for it); chattering at a butterfly or the cursor through the window.
- **Fox**: pouncing nose first into the sand or snow after something heard (the mousing leap), head buried, tail up; caching a snack (burying it, nosing sand over it) and digging it up later; grooming her tails one by one; as the 霸道姊姊, herding the small ones back together when they wander off.
- **Owl**: turning its head right round (far, slowly) and bobbing it side to side to judge distance; puffing up big when startled; sleeping on one foot; hooting at dusk (the clock's evening); swallowing a snack whole with a gulp.
- **Chick**: following whoever is nearest in a line (imprinting), the chick hurrying after; dust bathing in the beach's sand, wriggling and fluffing; falling asleep standing and nodding off forward; peeping louder when left alone.
- **Duckling**: dabbling (tipping bottom-up in the pond, tail in the air); ducklings in a row behind the duck if there are several (or behind the chick, mixed up); preening water off; waddling at speed to the pond the moment it appears.
- **Together**: grooming each other (cats, the fox and a cat), a dog play-bowing to a cat that ignores it, the owl watching from up high and hooting at whoever squabbles, everyone piling up asleep in one heap when it is late.

### Roger's twenty-second pass (2026-11-16)

Items 1 and 2 built the same day; item 3 waits for Roger's answers below, and comes with the twentieth pass's item 1 (the fox's lag), whose (b) it replaces.

1. **The settings' pets page and adopting** (「`領養新寵物` 按鈕位置」「進去 `領養新寵物` 的畫面高度不夠導致 `先選一隻吧` 難找」「點選寵物卡片時，毛色選擇可以放在卡片選擇就好」). The adopt button was the last chip of a row of names that scrolls sideways: with the seven pets a new desk starts with, it sat out of sight past the right edge (and a mouse wheel does not scroll that row). Adopting, the seven cards stood three a row, 196 px tall, so the coats and the button came under the fold.

   **Built (2026-11-16)**: the page has a title row, 「我的寵物」 on the left and 「＋ 領養新寵物」 as a button on the right, always in sight; the names wrap onto rows (`FlexboxLayout`, Slint 1.18) instead of scrolling. Adopting, the same row reads 「領養新寵物」 with 「算了」 (back to the pet chosen before; not shown with no pets yet) and 「帶牠回家」 (greyed 「先點一隻吧」 until a card is picked) at the top; the cards stand four a row, smaller (194 px), so all seven show at once. The coats are on the card: picked, its blurb gives way to its coat dots, and its portrait is drawn again in the coat picked (`settings::set_portrait`; a card left goes back to its first coat). The separate coat row and the button under the cards are gone. `settings_screenshots` now has the seven pets and an `adopt-fox` shot (the fox in 夜色 picked). Pictures `inbox/adopt.png`, `inbox/pets-row.png`.

2. **The dog's panting sounded like sand** (「狗吐舌頭的音效是沙沙聲有點怪」). `Sound::Pant` (the Shiba's and the fox's pant: fidgets, a good scratch, loving a hold) was a band of plain noise at 1.1–1.5 kHz, eight bursts: its weight sat near 2.5 kHz, a fifth of it above 3 kHz, a hiss.

   **Built (2026-11-16)**: breath only, shaped by an open mouth: each breath through three soft formants (out a warm "hah", 560/1150/2300 Hz, 0.13 s; in a thinner, quieter "hih", 700/1700/2900 Hz, 0.1 s), swelling in and fading out, nine of them, each a little different. Its weight is now near 1.3 kHz, under a tenth above 3 kHz. Tried and dropped: a voice mixed under the breath (it squeaked, or grated). Gemini 3.6/3.8 Flash were asked to compare takes and did not agree with themselves from one call to the next, so Roger's ear decides: `inbox/pant-old.wav`, `inbox/pant-new.wav`. If synthesis cannot get there, a CC0 recording as the calls got (fifteenth pass, item 5): nine CC0 candidates found on Freesound ("dog panting"), not cut or screened yet (no ffmpeg on the box that may be run; bake.py needs one).

3. **The fox's tails still do not flow, and a new look for her** (「九尾尾巴還是不夠飄逸，可參考以下。另外當作九尾新造型切換」, with three Gemini pictures in `inbox/`: 三視圖 (beast and human forms, front, side and back), 平常 (snow-white, blue markings) and 施法狀態 (the same, its tails turned to flame, red markings)). Now her tails are nine straight tubes ringed with ridges, standing up like fingers from one point, each the same thickness to a pink tip, swaying stiffly. In the pictures:
   - **Each tail a plume**: thin at the root, widest about 60% along (near the head's width), flattened rather than round, ending in a fine tip that flicks outward; an S along it (up from the root, out, the tip curling back); a ragged, tufted edge of fur, not rings.
   - **The fan**: the nine spread in a half circle behind her like an open flower, the top ones upright, the outer ones sweeping out sideways and drooping almost to the floor, overlapping in layers; the whole fan about two and a half times her body's width from the front, a long trailing cascade from the side.
   - **Flowing**: each tail a chain of 5–6 bones on springs, each bone lagging the one before, so a wave runs from root to tip; each tail its own phase and length; trailing behind and rippling as she walks, overshooting and settling when she stops, lifting and fanning out when she casts.
   - **Plan**: new tails from `gen_tails.py` (plume profile, flattened section, S spine, tufted edge from the fur shader's strands and a jagged silhouette in the mesh), spring chains in `skin.rs` (as the ears and cheeks already bounce), and the fan's layout redone from the three views. Built to cost no more than now: fewer rings along each tail but flatter, so it also does the twentieth pass's 1 (b) (her 2.2–2.8 ms a frame against 1.3–1.7 ms for the others). Its 1 (a) (the budget fair in frames, not time) first, so her frame rate is measured before and after.
   - **The new look, 「雪狐」** (from 平常): pure white fur, ice-blue markings (a three-leaf crest on the forehead, three curved strokes on each cheek), blue eyes, a silver-white cord with a silver bell, ears pink inside, the tails white with a faint sparkle of frost. Chosen as any coat is (毛色, and on her card when adopting).
   - **Casting** (from 施法狀態): while she casts (狐火), her tails glow from white at the root through gold and orange to red at the tips, flickering like flame, with sparks; a ring of light round her neck; the markings turn red. Questions for Roger: (a) the flame tails for every coat, or the snow look's own turn (the current white coat already is red and gold, much like the picture); (b) the **human form** (人型形態: a chibi child with fox ears, long silver hair, a white kimono with a bow and bell, the nine tails behind): a switch between her two forms would need a whole new two-legged model (body, hair, clothes, a walk, hands, its own sitting and lying), weeks of work, far more than a coat. Recommended: the beast form's tails and the snow look first; the human form as its own later item, if wanted.

#### Roger's answers on item 3, and his third log (2026-11-16)

- **Flame tails when casting**: 「只給九尾就好了」, read as: the fox alone casts, so the flame tails are hers in every coat (no other species gets them).
- **The human form: yes**, with two more pictures (`inbox/…3D示意與局部拆解圖.jpeg`, `…拆解圖2.jpeg`): a chibi child, big head, fox ears, three hairdos (one ponytail with a thin red ribbon, two bunches, 公主頭 half up), a short white 古風 robe with embroidered edges, a sash with a big bow and a bell (silver with blue for the 銀白 look, red and gold braid with a gold bell for the 紅髮 look), bare feet; face marks as the beast's; the nine tails as one piece at a tail root behind. The second picture splits it into two themes: 銀白髮 / 藍 and 火紅髮 / 紅金 (hair, eyes and marks red, the hair's ends fading to gold). The beast form there is rounder still, legs very short, a separate tail fan joined at a ball at the root.
- **Order, decided**: the twentieth pass's 1 (a) (frames shared fairly) → the new tails (plumes on spring chains, its 1 (b)) → the 雪狐 coat and the flame tails when casting → the human form as its own piece of work (a two-legged model: rig, mesh, walk, sit, lie, hands, hair and robe that swing; switched between in her menu with a puff of fox-fire), planned in detail when the tails are done.
- **His third log** (`inbox/chonkgene.log`, 3.8 h, seven pets, 25%): the same as the second: the fox 17.8 frames a second shown of 40.9 asked (43%), the others 17–28 of 22–41 (64–78%); her frames 2.68 ms, theirs 1.32–1.74. And nine double-clicks in five seconds on the sleeping fox, each turned down 「asleep」.

**Built (2026-11-16)**:
- **The twentieth pass's 1 (a), frames shared fairly**: `gpu::Budget` splits the budget among the pets drawing in proportion to what their frames cost (`User::per_frame`, eased from each frame's asked cost), not in equal time, so a costly pet gets as many frames as the rest. With the log's costs and asks (test `a_costly_pet_gets_as_many_frames_as_the_rest`): before, the fox 13.6 frames a second against 21–28; now all about 21, the GPU share the same (0.26 for 25%: the shares fade, and the burst). Owed: a log from Roger's machine to see it there.
- **Hugged asleep**: a double-click (or 抱抱) on a sleeping pet picks it up in a 搖搖抱 without waking it: no line, no face, it sleeps on in your hands and is put down asleep (`Pet::hug_you`; a 環抱 or 舉高高 asked from the menu while it sleeps is still turned down). `CHONKGENE_STAGE=hug-asleep` puts the first pet to sleep and hugs it 3 s later (seen under Xvfb: the Shiba cradled on its back, the sleep bubble going, asleep after); `examples/touch.rs` has a `hug-asleep` scene.
- Release notes: the twentieth pass's 2–4 (the child's hands and the 抱抱… page, the 場景… page, the gallop) were missing or out of date; written in.

**Built (2026-11-16): the new tails, first cut** (the beast form's; the 雪狐 coat, the flame tails and the human form still to come):
- **Shape** (`fox/gen_tails.py`, spliced into `quad.ron`): nine plumes in a half circle behind her, 128° either side of upright, all leaning back; each thin at the root, widest at 0.58 of its length (near her head's width), flattened a little (`flat` 1.2), easing to a tip that stays thick enough to see it turn; the centre line an S across the fan (out and down from the root, back through the middle, the tip flicking up toward upright like a flame's). The first try turned each tip "outward", which for the side tails is the way they already point, so nothing turned: the curl is now across the tail, in the fan. The outer ones droop to her knees, no lower: under `FLOOR + 0.5` they would count as standing on the floor and spread her shadow (`mesh.rs`, `reach`); the generator prints the lowest point. Five bones a tail (55 bones of 64), three cones a bone (135 parts, 144 before). 56 363 vertices against 56 100: not lighter, as hoped for the twentieth pass's 1 (b), but no heavier; the fair budget above is what gives her the frames. Her picture is 6.2 × 4.1 (5.8 × 3.8 before, 16% more area) so the fan is not cut off walking or in a gesture.
- **Flowing** (`skin.rs`, several tails only): her swish 0.65 as fast, each segment 0.85 rad behind the one before (`FLOW_LAG`; 1.25 lined the middle up a whole swish late, which the seaweed test reads as in step); each segment pushed against the way the one before it moves (`FLOW_PUSH` 10: it lags, then whips past; 40 piled up down the chain and crumpled the tails into a ball), on springs softening from 3.2 Hz at the root to 1.5 at the tip, damped 0.32; the fan swept back 14° at full pace, swung by a change of pace (`FLOW_SWING`); asleep they fold over her on their own slower spring (`FLOW_FOLD_HZ` 0.5, behind her lying down: folding with her, the tips jumped 10.7 px a frame), folded further (`BLANKET_TIP` 74°, 62 before) to reach her back.
- Review: `inbox/fox-tails.gif` (old above, new below, walking, turning, a poke), `inbox/fox-tails-views.png` (front, three quarters, side, back), `inbox/fox-tails-poses.png` (sitting, lying down asleep, the fox-fire scene). Owed: Roger's look; asleep, the outer tails roll up behind her a little like shrimp (to tune with his look); the tufted edge of the pictures is not there yet (the fur shader's strands only).

**Roger, the same day: which picture for what.** The beast form's tails and bell after `…3D示意與局部拆解圖.jpeg`; the human form's tails, hair and clothes after `…3D示意與局部拆解圖2.jpeg`.

**Built (2026-11-16): the beast's tails and bell after his picture of her parts.**
- **Tails as a lotus**: in that picture (九尾巴（分件）, 九尾分件與基部銜接點) the nine are fat teardrops of fluff packed like petals round a ball at the tail root, not the long plumes of the first cut. Now each is 1.9 long (2.45), fullest at 0.42 of its length with a half-width of 0.43 (0.32), narrow at the root and drawn to a point that flicks up; the fan 112° either side of upright (128°), so they overlap; a round puff at the tail root they grow out of (`rootpuff`, on the hips). Lighter too: 44 807 vertices (56 100 before any of this), which does the twentieth pass's 1 (b) after all.
- **The bell on its cord** (銀色/金色與小鈴鐺, 頸部與腰部鈴鐺細節): the cord was buried in her ruff; now thicker and further out (a torus 0.48 round, 0.075 thick), so the collar shows round her neck; twisted, two strands: a stripe of a second colour every 20° (a new slot `braid`: gold on the white coat's red cord, as the red and gold braid in the picture; silver grey on 夜色's white rope); the bell a little bigger (0.16), raised so its bottom stays above `FLOOR + 0.5` (lower, it widened her shadow, `every_mesh_pet_fits_the_shader_and_has_a_walk_and_a_shadow`). The gold stripes are faint at desktop size.

**Plan: her human form (人型形態)**, from `…拆解圖2.jpeg` (and the 三視圖, 平常 and 施法狀態 pictures). Not started; for Roger to read before it is.
- **What she is**: a chibi child about as tall as she is now on all fours with her tails up (so her window hardly changes): a big round head (about half her height), fox ears on top, big eyes with the beast's marks (a three-leaf crest on the forehead, three strokes on each cheek), a small round body, short arms with little hands, short legs and bare feet (短短的腳丫); the nine tails behind her as one fan at a root at the small of her back, the lotus of her beast form.
- **Two looks, one to each of her coats** (the picture's 雙色版): 銀白版 for 雪狐 (silver-white hair, blue eyes and marks, the robe's edges embroidered in silver, a silver rope tied in a bow at the waist with a silver bell, tail tips pale blue) and 紅髮版 for the white coat she has now (red hair fading to gold at the ends, red eyes and marks, the robe's edges red and gold, a red and gold braided sash in a bow with a gold bell, tails white through gold to orange). 夜色 and 金色 get theirs in the same way (hair and trim from the coat's colours).
- **Clothes**: a short white 古風小長衫, crossed collar, wide sleeves to the wrist, the hem above the knees, an embroidered border at the hem, cuffs and collar; the sash with its big bow and bell at the front.
- **Hair, three to choose from** (as a coat is chosen, in her card): 綁單馬尾 (one ponytail high at the back, a thin red ribbon), 綁雙馬尾 (two bunches), 公主頭 (long, half tied up at the back). Hair is the hardest part to make look right and to keep cheap: long hair and a ponytail need bones of their own to swing, as the tails do (springs).
- **How**: a third kind of model beside the four-legged and the birds: `assets/species/fox/human.ron`, a two-legged rig of its own (hips, spine, head, two arms with hands, two legs with feet, ears, the nine tails, hair bones), baked and skinned the same way; the robe and the hair as parts of the mesh (no cloth simulation: the sleeves, hem and hair swing on springs, as the ears and tails do). Her walk on two legs (a toddler's: short quick steps, arms swinging), sitting (on the floor, legs out), lying asleep (on her side, curled under her tails), and her gestures (a wave, a stretch, casting with her hands held up, as in 施法狀態).
- **Switching**: her menu gets 「變身」 (and the tray's pet menu): a puff of fox-fire, and she is the other form; the form is saved with her (`PetSave`), so she wakes up as she was. Asleep, or busy, as with the scenes.
- **What carries over**: her lines, mind, personality, scenes and spells (the human form casts with her hands); touches by zone (head, cheeks, belly, hands, tail; no paws); hugs (held under the arms, cradled); the squabbles with the others. What needs its own work: every pose the four-legged rig has (sit, lie, sleep poses, the belly-up laugh, the slide and glide, the pond, the beach, the dance), so this is several passes long; built in this order: the model and its look, standing and walking, the switch, sitting and sleeping, touches and hugs, then the scenes one by one.
- **Questions for Roger before it starts**: (1) the looks tied to her coats as above, or the hair and theme picked on their own; (2) does she change form only when asked, or now and then by herself too (as she casts); (3) in human form, does she still go on all fours anywhere (running, the slide), or always on two legs.
- **Built (2026-11-16): 雪狐 and the flame tails.** A coat `snow` (雪狐; Roger's 平常 picture and the 銀白 look): near-white fur, ice-blue marks and forehead flame, blue eyes, pink inside the ears with no red tips, a white twisted rope (silver-grey `braid`) and a silver bell, the tail tips pale blue. Casting (`PoseParams::glow`, as her face's glow), in every coat (Roger: 只給九尾), her tails burn: white at the root, gold from about a third of the way, orange past half, red at the tips, the colours licking up and down as a flame flickers (`mesh.wgsl` `flame_tails`, noise rising with the clock, now passed in `mouth_r.w`), over the fur's own light and shade. The mesh marks each vertex on a tail of several, and how far along it, in `mouth.w` (negative; `mesh.rs`), so no new vertex stream. Coat names 金色 and 夜色 showed as 其他 in settings: named. `examples/touch.rs` `GLOW=1` casts all through.
- **Roger's answers (2026-11-16)**: (1) not tied: 「我之後會變化髮型、和顏色（預設白色）更換」: the hairdo and the hair's colour are chosen on their own (in her card, beside her coat), white by default; the looks above are where the colours come from, not a pairing. (2) 「她偶爾會自己變，我也可以叫他維持原型和人型」: now and then she changes by herself (as she casts by herself); her menu has 「變身」 and a choice to keep her as she is: 隨她變 (the default), 維持原型, 維持人型 (saved with her). (3) 「不會」: in human form she is always on two legs: she runs on two legs, and does not slide on her belly (a run and a little skid on her feet instead).
- **Built (2026-11-17): the model and its look, first cut** (`assets/species/fox/human.ron`; not in the app yet). A chibi child as tall as the beast form with her tails up (2.6 units, the head and ears about half), 61 of the shader's 64 bones: hips, body, head, two arms, two legs, the bell, two ears, three hair bones (`hair_b` for the ponytail or the long hair, `hair_l`/`hair_r` for the bunches; hairdos never share), the nine tails' 45, two jiggles (belly, chin). The robe is a skirt (an ellipsoid with a torus rim at the hem: a round cone ends in a ball and hid her legs), a chest with the crossed collar painted on, wide sleeves with a border at the cuff, a braided sash with a big bow, its ends and the bell under it; bare legs and feet. The head is a sphere with a small mound for the mouth, the beast's face painted on (the same eyes bigger and lower, the three leaves, the cheek strokes, 麻呂眉, the liner); a hair cap leaving the face, a fringe of pointed strands from a centre parting (the middle left bare for the leaves), locks beside the cheeks; fox ears the colour of her hair, pink inside. The tails are `gen_tails.py human`: the lotus 0.8 as fat, 1.55 long, from the small of her back, the fan 96° either side (narrower and they lie on the floor and spread her shadow). Coats carry the beast's names (白色 = the 紅髮版's red and gold trim and gold bell, 雪狐 = the 銀白版's silver trim, white rope and silver bell, 金色, 夜色), sixteen colour slots at most (the shaders hold sixteen: twenty-one first made her eyes black and her marks blue).
  - **Two new model fields**: a part's `style` (the hairdo it belongs to, `Model::styles` and `Model::styled`, the first one if unknown) and `hairs` (colours for a few slots laid over any coat, `Model::dyed`): `skin::human(id, style, hair)`. Hairdos: `ponytail` (綁單馬尾, the first), `twintails` (綁雙馬尾), `princess` (公主頭, long to her waist, long locks in front of her shoulders); hair colours `white` (the first), `red` (fading to gold at the ends), `gold`, `night`.
  - **Looking at her**: `cargo run --release -p chonkgene-render --example mesh -- still fox-human <dir> [scale] [coat]` with `STYLE=` and `HAIR=` writes her front, three quarters, side and back as the app's renderer draws them. Pictures for Roger: `inbox/fox-human.png` (銀白版: ponytail, white hair, 雪狐; 紅髮版: ponytail, red hair, 白色; bunches in white; 公主頭 in red) and `inbox/fox-human-hair.png` (the three hairdos in the four hair colours, from the front).
  - **Test**: `the_fox_human_form_fits_the_shader_and_stands_on_her_feet` (the hairdos and hair colours listed, bones and slots fit, the face on parts every hairdo has, her shadow under her feet, a hair colour on every coat, an unknown hairdo falls back).
  - **Owed**: Roger's look; the hair is smooth shapes (no strands carved in yet); from behind, the long hair is mostly hidden by the tails (as in the 三視圖); the hem rim reads a little like a ring. **Touches, when they come**: as a child, her zones are the head, cheeks, hands and tails only: no belly or bottom pokes, no tickling (the old §9 note's rule, kept). Next in the order above: standing and walking.
- **Roger, the same day: 「確實不夠完美 看起來還很粗糙 不夠萌，跟我提供的3d稿子還是有很大的差距」**: compare her with `…拆解圖2.jpeg`, the seal, the tails, everything. Compared side by side (his 銀白 and 紅髮 heads, the robe, the faces) and **reworked** the same day:
  - **The model is now written by `assets/species/fox/gen_human.py`** (then `gen_tails.py human` spliced in), since hair strands, borders and the collar have to be placed against the head's and robe's surfaces. Hair is built three ways: `sheet` (flat locks of overlapping flat ellipsoids along the head: the fringe, the locks), `tube` (round bunches: capsules end to end along a Catmull-Rom curve; ellipsoids left ridges where they met, round cones a ball at each end) and `strand` (the clumps at the back).
  - **Face**: wide mochi face (head 0.72 × 0.61, round cheeks out at the sides where nothing is painted, as paint only lands within 0.05 of the head part), a button nose; **chibi eyes** (`face.chibi`, face.rs `chibi_eye` = paint.wgsl `chibi_eye`): the white, a gradient iris dark at the top with a darker rim, a big pupil, a heavy upper lash line flicking up at the outer corner, two highlights the same way on both eyes; **the seal at rest** (`face.seal`, `seal_marks` in both): a drop with a round hollow and a curling wing either side on the forehead, a plump leaf over each brow, three short stripes under each eye's outer corner, thick at the outer end; no 麻呂眉 dots. One more uniform row (`style`: chibi, seal) in pet.wgsl and mesh.wgsl.
  - **Hair**: the cap set back so the forehead shows; a centre-parted curtain fringe and strands falling over the forehead's sides (the middle bare for the crest); locks at the face's edge at eye level, the cheeks in front of them below (the first try had them over the cheeks, a hood); white hair a cool grey-blue white (#E9EDF5, tips #CCD6E6) so it reads against the white robe and tails.
  - **Ears** bigger (0.68 tall, 0.34 at the foot, tilted 28° out), five white fluff cones inside.
  - **Robe**: a new shape `Frustum` (sdf.rs, pet.wgsl, kind 4: a capped cone with rounded rims; a round cone ends in a ball) for an A-line robe with a flat hem and trumpet sleeves; a thick hem border with gold lines and a scroll of gold dots; the lapels as broad bands into the front border; braided bands round the upper arms; a wide sash with a red rope braided with gold, a bow of two rope rings, the bell, two tassels; hands out of the cuffs.
  - **Tails**: 白色's are white through gold to orange, as the 紅髮版's.
  - **Tests**: `a_frustum_has_flat_ends_and_rounded_rims`, `the_human_forms_seal_and_eyes_land_in_the_same_place_on_the_gpu_and_the_cpu`.
  - Pictures: `inbox/fox-human.png`, `inbox/fox-human-hair.png`, `inbox/fox-human-vs-ref.png` (his heads beside hers). Still short of the picture: no strand grooves in the hair, the fringe's edge a little lumpy, the ponytail plainer than his.
- **Built (2026-11-17): standing and walking** (still only in the `mesh` example; the app gets her with the switch). Her rig has `leg_l` and `leg_r`, so `Mover` gives her the birds' two-legged walk (`Waddle`, her `gait`: swing 24°, lift 0.08, roll 4°, about 3.3 quick steps a second); what a child's body adds is `Waddle::child`, which does nothing on a bird (no such bones):
  - **Arms** (`arm_l`, `arm_r`): each swings with the other side's leg, 24° either way at full pace, smoothed on a spring (a planted foot sweeps back evenly, an arm swings); out a little at each step; at rest they hang, stirring with her breath.
  - **Tails**: the four-legged fox's flowing wave, now shared: `Tail::chains` and `Tail::wave` (skin.rs) out of `Walk`, which calls them as before. Streaming out behind at pace, flung by turns, flicked by a poke; asleep, laughing, melted and pleased as the beast's.
  - **Hair** (`hair_b`, `hair_l`, `hair_r`) on springs: hanging back against the head's lean, streaming back at pace, bouncing with the steps, flung out by turns. **Ears** bob a beat behind the steps; the **bell** swings on its cord as the beast's.
  - **Test**: `the_fox_child_swings_her_arms_and_her_tails_and_hair_flow`; the planted-foot test now takes her too (slides at most 1.6 px, as the birds).
  - Picture: `inbox/fox-human-walk.gif` (雪狐 with white hair and a ponytail; 白色 with red hair in bunches: walking, turning, standing).
  - Not yet hers: sitting is still the birds' (legs folded under, which hides them); gestures move only what a bird has (no arms in them yet). Next: the switch (變身 and the lock), then sitting and sleeping.
- **Roger, the same day: 「這個人型真的跟原稿差很多」**, asking whether to remake her in Blender or have Gemini draw a more detailed 3D view. Answer: the gap is the method, not the pictures. Blended round shapes baked on a 2.5 cm grid cannot make hair strands, sharp edges or tapering tips, however they are tuned; more pictures would only give a more exact melted wax. Blender (4.0.2 on the box, run headless from Python) makes real meshes. Gemini's image models need billing on the key (free tier by Roger's choice), so the orthographic head turnaround could not be generated here. **Pilot, built (2026-11-17): her hair in Blender, the 綁單馬尾 head only**:
  - **`assets/species/fox/hair_blend.py`** (`blender -b --factory-startup -P hair_blend.py -- ponytail <out.mesh>`): the main mass is one sheet over a shell round the head, grooved along the way it is combed to the knot (the grooves fading where they meet), its lower edge brought in onto the head; over it, clumps with a lens-shaped cross-section (full on top, flatter under, sharp at the edges) swept along smooth paths and tapering to points: the fringe fanning from a parting on her right near the crown (in order round the fan, so no two cross; the forehead's middle bare for the crest), the locks framing the face to the chin, the knot high on the back of her head (seen over her head from the front, as in the picture), the ponytail hanging to the nape in six pointed clumps, a thin red ribbon (band, two loops, two ends). Blender's BVH gives each vertex its occlusion; the grooves are shaded darker in the hair's own colour through it (a second colour in them striped the red hair gold). About 26k vertices, under 2 s.
  - **`chonkgene_render::mesh::import`** adds such a mesh (text: `v` lines with position, normal, two colour slots and a mix, gloss, fuzz, occlusion and up to four bones by name; `f` lines) to a baked one; colours come from the coat, so dyes apply. **`Model::only`** leaves out the parts it replaces (the face stays on its part; `Model::styled` now uses it). The `mesh` example takes `HAIR_MESH=<file>` and bakes without the hair parts. Test: `a_mesh_made_elsewhere_joins_the_baked_one`.
  - White hair's tip colour darker (`#B2BDD1`, was `#CCD6E6`) for the tips' shading.
  - Picture for Roger: `inbox/fox-human-blender.png` (his 銀白版, the old cut and the Blender one from the front, three quarters, behind and the side; his 紅髮版 beside hers).
  - Not done: the other hairdos (twin tails, 公主頭); the ears (still blended shapes); the face (still the blended head, wider than his); the robe; wiring it into the app (a model field naming the mesh per hairdo, the mesh checked in). Decide after Roger's look whether to carry on this way.

### Roger's twenty-third pass (feedback only, 2026-11-16; to do next, from 2026-11-17)

1. **Japanese and English** (「要考慮支援日文和英文語系」). Now everything is in Traditional Chinese: the pets' lines (`assets/lines/zh-TW.ron`, the only file there), the menus and bubbles' fixed words in Rust, the settings window (`ui/settings.slint`), the tray, the release notes. Plan to write up first: a language setting (follow the system, or chosen in 一般), lines files `ja.ron` and `en.ron` beside `zh-TW.ron` (the every-species and length tests run on each; the 14-character limit becomes a width limit for English), UI strings out of the code into one table per language (Slint's own `@tr` and its translations, or our table passed in), the voice rules of PLAN §1.11 for each language (牠/牠們 has no Japanese or English twin; a glossary each), and who writes them (draft by Claude, Gemini as a second opinion, Roger's look; a native reader for Japanese if he can find one). Fonts: jf open 粉圓 has kana and Latin, so one font may do for all three (to check its kanji cover for Japanese).

   **Plan, written up (2026-11-17)**, for Roger to decide on before building:
   - **What there is to translate**: 490 pet lines (`zh-TW.ron`, every species' voice, `{you}` and `{name}` filled in) and 318 fixed strings in the code (82 in `settings.slint`, 81 in `app.rs` (menus, bubbles' fixed words), 57 in `settings.rs`, 28 `gpu_check.rs`, 27 `species.rs` (names, labels), 21 the tray, a few more); the release notes; the README.
   - **The font**: checked: jf open 粉圓 2.1 has 174 of the 176 kana (the two missing are archaic), all of ASCII, and every kanji tried (変, 尻, 桜, 眠, 撫…): one font for all three languages, nothing to add.
   - **The language**: by default the system's (Windows' display language, Linux `LANG`): Japanese → 日本語, Chinese of any kind → 繁體中文, anything else → English; a choice in 一般 (語言: 跟著系統 / 繁體中文 / 日本語 / English), each name written in its own language; switching takes effect at once for menus and lines, and reopens the settings window.
   - **Where the strings go**: one table per language for all fixed strings, `i18n/zh-TW.po`, `ja.po`, `en.po` (gettext, the format translators and their tools know), the Chinese as the key (the code keeps reading as now). Slint reads them with its own `@tr("…")` (bundled at build, `slint::select_bundled_translation`); the Rust side looks up the same files through a small `tr("…")` (parsed at build into a table; no gettext library). A test fails when a string in the code has no entry in a table.
   - **The lines**: `ja.ron` and `en.ron` beside `zh-TW.ron`, the same keys; the every-species test and `only_for` run on each; the 14-character limit becomes a width limit measured in the font (English runs wider). Pet names: the default names translated (阿柴 → ja しばお / en Shibby, 小九 → ja きゅう / en Kyu, …, to choose with Roger); a name you typed stays as typed.
   - **The voice** (PLAN §1.11 has the Chinese rules): each language gets its own short guide: Japanese in plain, soft speech, each species its own habit (the fox a haughty big sister, おねえさん with ～わよ; the Shiba stubborn and blunt; the cats aloof; the chick short and quick, the duckling slow); English short, simple, cute without baby talk, 牠/牠們 has no twin and is just "it/they" in the UI; a glossary each (戳 → poke/つつく, 抱抱 → hug/だっこ, 肉肉 → …).
   - **Who writes them**: Claude drafts every string and line from the Chinese with the voice guide; Gemini reads them back as a second opinion (does it sound natural, is it cute, does it fit the species); Roger looks over a page of samples per species before the rest; for Japanese a native reader if Roger knows one (recommended: the cuteness lives in the endings).
   - **Order**: (a) the tables and `tr`/`@tr` with Chinese only (nothing changes on screen; the test catches strings left in the code); (b) the language setting; (c) English UI + lines (easier to check); (d) Japanese UI + lines; (e) README and release notes in each.
   - **Questions for Roger**: (1) default by the system's language as above, or always 繁體中文 until changed; (2) translate the default pet names or keep them as they are (阿柴 in an English menu); (3) does he read Japanese well enough to judge cuteness, or is there someone who can; (4) the release notes and README in three languages, or Chinese and English only.
   - **Roger's answers (2026-11-17)**: (1) follow the computer; (2) my pick: translated, for pets adopted from now on (a saved name is never changed); (3) Claude writes the Japanese and people's reactions will correct it; (4) the README stays English (the release notes are in Chinese now: to ask).

   **Built (2026-11-17)**:
   - **The tables**: `chonkgene_core::i18n` (`Lang` zh-TW / ja / en, `tr`, `trf` with `{key}`s, `mark` for `const`s, `set`, `current`, `Lang::for_locale`); the Chinese in the code is the key; `i18n/<lang>/LC_MESSAGES/chonkgene.po` read by Rust at start (a small parser, no gettext library) and by Slint (`@tr`, bundled at build, `select_bundled_translation`); a string not translated shows in Chinese. 230 strings: the pet menu, scenes, coats, species, names, the adoption cards, the body-size lines, the settings window, the tray (platform now depends on core for `tr`). `tools/i18n/extract.py` gathers them into the .po files (keeping translations); `tools/i18n/fill.py <lang> tools/i18n/<lang>.txt` writes translations in from a plain list (`中文 → translation`); `tests/i18n.rs` fails on any shown string missing from a .po. The `gpu-check` report and log lines stay as they were (for developers).
   - **The language**: `Settings::language` (empty: the computer's, by `sys-locale`: ja → 日本語, zh → 繁體中文, else English); 一般 › 語言 (跟著電腦 / 繁體中文 / 日本語 / English, each name in itself); changing it redraws the settings window at once (Slint's words, and the cards made in Rust) and the pet process makes its tray menu again (the pets' menus are made each time they open).
   - **English**: every string (`tools/i18n/en.txt`: chonkGene, Bond, the den, mochi, chaos mode; default names Shibby, Chai, Taro, Hoot, Yolky, Dumpling, Kyu) and every line (`assets/lines/en.ron`, each species' voice: woof, stammers, "…", hoo, peep, quack, the fox's "Sis" and "Hmph"); ASCII "..." in English (粉圓's "…" sits in the middle of the line, as Chinese wants).
   - **Japanese**: the same (`tools/i18n/ja.txt`, `assets/lines/ja.ron`: ワン, 結巴, 「……」, ホー, ピヨ, クワ, the fox's お姉さん and ～わよ; names しばまる, チャイ, タロ, ふくまる, きみ, だんご, きゅう). Gemini 3.6 Flash read the lines back; taken: おどろおどろ (eerie) → おどろ、おどろ, むちむち (has a sexy ring) → ぷにぷに, まあまあ when patted, pauses before クワ, the Munchkin's stammer, the fox's tsundere; not taken where it went against the Chinese.
   - **Lines by language** (`lines.rs`): `ja.ron` and `en.ron` beside `zh-TW.ron`; a line missing in a language is said in Chinese; the tests (every species every line, width) run on each language written; the length limit is now a width: as wide as 14 Chinese characters in 粉圓 at bubble size, with the widest names filled in (eight W's in English).
   - **Adoption cards**: four equal columns whatever the language (a long name or blurb, or the coat dots, made one column wider): each card a quarter of the page (`root.width` less sidebar, padding and gaps); names elide; the coat dots a little smaller. 日本語 calls the Fold スコティッシュ (the full name did not fit).
   - Pictures: `inbox/lang-{pets,general,adopt}-{en,ja}.png`. Owed: Roger's look on Windows (the tray, a menu, bubbles in each language); a native reader's look at the Japanese, as reactions come.
2. **One font for the bubbles and the UI** (「對話框字體是不是和 ui 介面的字體不一樣，可考慮一律用 ui 字體」). Yes, they differ: bubbles use 芫荽 Iansui, falling back to 粉圓 for what it lacks (`text::Face::bubble`); menus, labels and the settings window use jf open 粉圓 (`Face::ui`, `settings.slint`). Plan: bubbles in 粉圓 too (`Face::bubble` → 粉圓 only), the bubble's size and padding checked again (粉圓 is rounder and a little wider), and 芫荽 dropped from the build if nothing else uses it (smaller download; `licenses/` updated). Small; can go first.

   **Built (2026-11-17)**: `text::Face::bubble` is gone; bubbles, menus and labels all use `Face::ui` (粉圓). A bubble is the same width and 3 px shorter (`tests/golden/bubble-*.png` redone); the padding needed no change. `assets/fonts/Iansui-Regular.ttf` (9.4 MB, included in the binary) and `licenses/Iansui-OFL.txt` removed, so the program is about 9 MB smaller.

3. **The fox's seal marks, at rest and casting** (Roger: 未施法狀態 after `…施法狀態.jpeg`, 施法狀態 after `…3D示意與局部拆解圖2.jpeg`). Now (painted in `face.rs`, glowing by `paint.wgsl` `spell_glow`): one red flame on the forehead, two short red strokes on each cheek, red eyeliner; casting, the face glows and seal lines grow longer.
   - **At rest** (the beast's face in 施法狀態.jpeg): on the forehead three small leaf-shaped petals fanned upward from one point (the middle one tallest), in the mark colour; two soft round brow dots (麻呂眉) above the eyes, in a pale fawn; on each cheek two long strokes tapering to a point and sweeping back toward the ear, the mark colour fading to orange; a short flick of eyeliner at the outer corner of each eye.
   - **Casting** (紅髮版配件 and 頭部 360° in 拆解圖2): the forehead mark grows into a crest: a tall flame drop in the middle with a round hollow in it, a curling flame wing either side of it making a trident (like a lotus seen from the front), and a leaf-shaped mark either side at the brows in place of the dots; the cheek strokes become three stacked tapering stripes each side; the eyeliner a full ring with a darker shadow, the iris the mark colour. The change grows in over the spell's first moments (the crest drawing itself up from the middle point) and fades back after, glowing as now.
   - In each coat's own mark colour (red on 白色, ice blue on 雪狐, and so on); for the human form the same marks on her face.

   **Built (2026-11-17)**: at rest (`face.rs` `fox_marks` on the CPU and `paint.wgsl` `fox_marks` on the GPU, the same shapes): three leaves fanned up on the forehead (the flame is gone), two long strokes along each cheek tapering back toward the ear and paling at their ends (they replace the two short stripes that were coat marks on the head part, coarse as the mesh), and 麻呂眉 brow dots (`face.brows`, a new slot `brow`: fawn on 白色, pale grey on 雪狐, cream on 金色, lavender on 夜色). Casting (`spell_marks`), the rest marks fade out as the seal grows in: the crest drawing itself up from its foot (a tall drop with a round hollow, a curling wing either side), a leaf over each brow dot, three stripes along each cheek drawn out toward the ears, a ring round each eye; in the mark's own colour, a soft glow round the seal but not over it (the old glow washed it pale). The eyeliner's flick drawn on up to the ear when casting is gone (not in the picture). `the_fox_marks_land_in_the_same_place_on_the_gpu_and_the_cpu` checks the two painters agree. Picture `inbox/fox-seal.png` (at rest, casting). Owed: the eyes while casting are still lit pale pink (the picture has them red); the human form's face.

### Roger's twenty-fourth pass (2026-11-17)

- **The 3D look is on hold** (「3d畫面還是差滿多，但先hold」): the Blender hair pilot stays as built (§8 "Plan: her human form"), to come back to.
- **Every tray command did nothing after the language changed** (found switching to Japanese with the settings window minimised; the right-click menu of the tray icon). Cause: tray-icon's and muda's event handlers live in a `OnceLock`, so `set_event_handler` works only the first time. The tray is made again on a change of language (its words); the remade menu's items get new ids, but the handler still held the first menu's ids and matched none of them. The left click (收起來 / 叫回來) kept working, as it needs no id. Every language switch did it, English and back to Chinese too. **Fixed (2026-11-17)**: the handlers are set once and read a table of the menu now up (`tray::Routes`, replaced each time the tray is made). Test `a_remade_tray_menu_still_sends_its_commands`. Checked with Windows clippy (stub C compiler); a try on Windows is owed. The pets' own menus were never affected: driven under Xvfb in Japanese and English (from the start, switched while running, sizes changed from the menu and from settings), every choice was carried out. Each choice in a pet's menu is now written to `chonkgene.log` (`menu: <action> (row n) for pet <id>`).
- **The pet's first page ran off the screen**: 19 rows (20 for the fox), 947 px tall at 100%, so 「結束」 was below a 900 px screen, and with Windows scaling at 125% or more 「設定…」 too. **Changed (2026-11-17)**: the five things to take out and play with (狗尾草, 丟球, 丟飛盤, 貓薄荷, 聚光燈) moved to a page of their own, **「一起玩…」** (いっしょに遊ぶ…, Play together...), as 「叫牠…」 and 「場景…」 are: 15 rows (16 for the fox), 771 px; and a page still taller than the work area is drawn smaller to fit (`menu::fit_scale`, not under half; test `a_menu_taller_than_the_screen_is_drawn_smaller_to_fit`). Checked under Xvfb on a 900 px and a 700 px screen (drawn at 0.91 there), clicks landing on the right rows. The tray's menu keeps them at its top level.
- **Steam**: asked whether chonkGene can go on Steam; the checklist is §11.
- **Next (Roger, 2026-11-17: 「明天用這份檔案使用 blender 進行開發看看」)**: model the human form in Blender from his new sheet `inbox/Gemini_Generated_Image_九尾人型(小)-3D示意與局部拆解圖.jpeg` (萌版九尾狐人型形態 3D開發結構及配件拆解圖). What it gives that the earlier pictures did not:
  - **Front, side and back of the whole body** on shared guide lines (head about 45% of her height, short legs, bare feet), the turnaround the pilot lacked: proportions can be measured, not guessed.
  - **The hair is long and straight in all three hairdos** (「三款直髮造型 (不綁辮子)」, front and back each): 綁單馬尾 with a red bow and the hair to the hips, 綁雙馬尾 long bunches, 公主頭 half up; a full side-swept fringe over the forehead, long locks in front of the ears to the chest. The pilot's short bob and short ponytail are wrong against it.
  - **The tail root**: a socket at the small of the back (腰椎尾部基部銜接點), the nine tails as nine separate pieces fanned from it (numbered 1–9).
  - **The robe laid out** (古風小長衫 結構與展開圖) in both themes, the waist bow as a separate piece (two styles), the collar rope with a bell at the neck as well as at the waist (頸部與腰部鈴鐺細節), the bare feet from front, side and below.
  - **Face**: the head from several sides and the seal marks as 2D decals (頭部 3D 多視角與面部咒印 2D 貼圖): eyes and marks painted, as ours are.
  - **Skeleton in T-pose**, front and side.
- **How (to start with)**: Blender scripts as the pilot (`hair_blend.py`), now for the whole figure: a measured turnaround first (her outline in front and side traced from the sheet into the script's numbers), then body and head as subdivided meshes, the hair as the grooved sheet plus long clumps, the robe with its borders; ours keeps painting the face. Compare with the sheet at every step, front, side and back, before going on. The app wiring (a model field naming the meshes per hairdo, the meshes checked in) only once Roger likes the look.

- **Started (2026-11-18): the whole figure in Blender, step 1 (body, robe, ears).** `assets/species/fox/figure_blend.py` (`blender -b --factory-startup -P figure_blend.py -- <out dir>`, about 2 s) traces her outline off the sheet's 正面 and 側面 (pixels to model units by one scale, K = 0.0034, the sole on FLOOR) and writes `figure.mesh` (bare legs, feet and hands as one metaball, so they join smoothly; the robe lofted from the traced outline with its crossed collar, the overlap's edge and the hem border standing proud; trumpet sleeves with bordered, turned-in cuffs; the sash with a braided three-strand rope, its two-loop bow, tassels and a slotted bell; the ears with fluff inside) and `human_figure.ron` (human.ron's coats, hairs and face with this figure's skeleton and only the painted head, which stays a baked part so our shaders keep painting the face). `mesh::import` now takes `z <zone>` lines, so one file carries the hands, robe and ears' touch zones. The mesh example draws it with `HUMAN_RON=<ron> BLEND_MESH=<mesh> … still fox-human`. Against the sheet (`inbox/fox-figure-body.png`, sheet left, ours right, front / side / back): head size and height, collar, sash, hem, sleeves and legs line up. Her head is now a third of her height, as drawn (the old model's was over half). Not yet: **the hair** (next: long straight hair in the three hairdos), the nine tails on their socket, the cuff and hem patterns, the bell on the collar; the seal's cheek strokes are placed for the old eyes and nearly touch the new ones (they need placing from the eyes in `face.rs` and `paint.wgsl`); the ears are plain. - **The tails' socket and the ears' set (2026-11-24, `go on`)**, in `figure_blend.py` (`tails()`, written into `figure.mesh`, so every hairdo has them):
  - **Where the root sits**: the turnaround's back view marks the socket (腰椎尾部基部銜接點) in a parting of the hair, above the sash; the 三視圖 (人型形態) and the 3D viewport sheet have the tails leave her back just under the long hair's ends, behind the top of the skirt, going back before they rise. Ours follows the second (`TAIL_ROOT_PY` 505, on the robe's back just under the sash, `tail_root()`): under the hair, every hairdo stays as built, and nothing has to part the hair.
  - **Nine plumes** (the sheet's 九尾巴, 獨立 9 分件), each its own piece: narrow at the root, fullest a little under half way (`tail_wide`, 0.17 half width), drawn to a point; flattened across the fan (`TAIL_FLAT`), seven shallow grooves down each (darkened as the hair's are); white at the root through `tailmid` to `tailtip` at the point (`tail_color`: two slots a vertex, so in two stretches), which in her coats gives 紅髮版 (`white`) white → gold → orange-red as drawn, and 銀白版 (`snow`) white → pale blue. Fanned 100° either side of upright (`tail_line`): each heads straight back from her first (clear of the hair), then out along the fan, which leans back (the middle ones most, so they lie behind the others, as petals overlap); out a little away from upright at the root, back toward it through the middle, the point flicking up. From behind they rise over the hair to about her shoulders; from the front the outer ones show either side of her, beyond the sleeves; from the side they sweep back, as the 三視圖's. Lowest point y −0.582 (kept above FLOOR + 0.5, or the shadow would count them as standing on the floor; printed by the script). A soft puff where the nine meet.
  - **Bones**: five a tail (`tail1` … `tail9`, `_b`, `_c`, `_d`, `_tip`, the first on `hips`), written into `human_figure.ron`'s pivots and parents (60 bones in all, of 64). So `Skeleton::tails` finds nine chains and the walk's `Tail::wave` moves them as it did the baked tails: tried in the mesh example's walk, no tearing.
  - **Imported tails burn when she casts**: `mesh::import` now marks tail vertices for the shader's flame (`tail_mark`, shared with the bake; it was only the baked tails' before). Test in `a_mesh_made_elsewhere_joins_the_baked_one`.
  - **Hair over the root**: the long hair hangs further back than the tails leave her, so its ends in the middle of her back came out in front of the root, seen from behind (the princess's V most). They now end no lower than the root there (`tail_hide()`: the curtain's middle strips and the princess's V lifted, their points kept); the tails hide that part anyway. Twintails has nothing there.
  - **Ears**: laid over the sheet's front and side views with the hair on (scratchpad `ear_overlay.py`), the tips were 0.04 low, a little in and back, the ears narrow. Now the tip at (0.425, head + 0.66, −0.06), the base 0.02 higher, `EAR_W` 0.225 (was 0.2): front and side outlines on the sheet's.

Each plume is a tube along that line at 60 rings (the beast form's 15 cones, four times finer), its cross-section as the round cones lie (`tail_frame`: wide along world x square to the line, squeezed `FLAT` 1.2 across), a round root and a round point; fuzz 1, gloss 0, as the baked fur is; coloured as the beast form's marks paint it (`tail_color`: a faint blush of `tailmid` past half way deepening to the end, then the last cone `tailtip`, sharp-edged); a plush puff at the root. Sized as the old `human` preset (length 1.55, width 0.8 of the beast form's, fanned 96°): lowest point y −0.564. 紅髮版 shows white plumes blushing gold with orange tips; 銀白版 white with pale blue tips. From behind the bouquet now covers her back up to her neck (the beast form's covers its body as much). The walk waves them without tearing. Same bones, so nothing in the code changed.

- **Her figure packed for the app (2026-11-24, `go on`)**: the first part of wiring her in.
  - **Lighter**: as made, her figure and a hairdo were 156 000 vertices and 308 000 triangles, more triangles than she has pixels at desktop size, and drew in 63 ms a frame on llvmpipe (the beast form 28). `tools/meshbake` (a tool of its own, like `iconbake`, so the app gets no simplifier) takes vertices away with meshoptimizer until the surface would move more than 0.003 (a fifth of a pixel at 100%); it never makes new ones, so those kept keep their colours, bones and woven-border marks; where two colours, zones, borders, shine or main bones meet the vertices stay put, so nothing bleeds; the normal, colour mix, shading and the place across a border weigh in. Figure 110 463 → 31 781 vertices, a hairdo about a quarter, the armbands two thirds: about 50 000 baked with her head, 32 ms a frame. Side by side at three times the app's size, no difference shows.
