  - **Packed**: `assets/species/fox/build_figure.sh` runs the Blender script, `meshbake` and `gzip -9n` into `assets/species/fox/figure/` (`figure.ron`, and `figure`, `armbands`, `hair_ponytail`, `hair_twintails`, `hair_princess` as `.mesh.gz`): 1.7 MB in all, embedded. The metaballs differ a little each run, so it runs only when the figure changes.
  - **The model names its meshes**: a model field `meshes` (`name`, a `style` for a hairdo's hair, `coats` for the armbands, which only 白色 wears); `Model::styles`/`styled` count and pick them as they do styled parts, `Model::meshes_on(coat)`, `Model::with_meshes` puts in what the app embeds. `mesh::bake` adds the look's meshes (unpacked with `flate2`, already in the tree through `png`; the lock file gains only the edge); `mesh::import` now also works out the shadow from all of it. `skin::human` / `human_model` are now this figure (`human.ron`, the blended model, stays as what `figure_blend.py` takes coats, hair colours and face from). The `mesh` example draws her from the embedded figure without `HUMAN_RON`/`BLEND_MESH`.
  - **Without a GPU**: the CPU draws sprites ray-marched from a model's parts, and her figure is meshes, so there she would be a head. Decided for the switch: on the sprite path she stays in her beast form and 「變身」 is not offered (a saved human form shows as the beast there, and comes back on a machine with a GPU).
  - Tests: `the_fox_human_form_fits_the_shader_and_stands_on_her_feet` (the meshes per look and coat, all embedded); the seal test compares her bare head (the ray-marcher draws no meshes).
  - **Next**: the switch: her save keeps the form, hairdo, hair colour and the lock (隨她變 / 維持原型 / 維持人型); 「變身」 in her menu, a puff of fox-fire, her window resized; now and then by herself; hairdo and hair colour in her card.
- **The switch, built (2026-11-24, `go on`)**: her human form in the app.
  - **Her save** (`PetSave`, all `#[serde(default)]`, so older saves load as before): `human` (her form now), `hairdo`, `hair` (unknown ones are the first: 綁單馬尾, white), `form_lock` (`FormLock`: `Free` 隨牠變, the default; `Beast` 維持原型; `Human` 維持人型). Only the pet process changes `human` (from the settings window's edits it keeps its own, unless the lock now keeps her in the other form).
  - **On the desktop**: `spawn` draws her from `skin::human(fox, hairdo, hair)` when she is in her human form and can take it (`morphs`: the fox, on a real GPU); everything else (mind, lines, touches, scenes) is the same pet. `App::morph` turns her: the save flips, her window is made again for the other form where she stood (`App::morphs`, placed in `sync_desktop` as 換班's newcomer is), with the casting ring of fox-fire and sparkles, and hello at once. Asked from the menu with her kept in one form, the lock follows to the form asked for.
  - **Her menu**: 叫牠… › 施法 ends with 「變身」 (the wand), its hint 「變成人型」 or 「變回原型」, where she can change (test `the_fox_changes_form_from_her_spells`).
  - **By herself** (隨牠變): about once an hour (half an hour to an hour and a half), when she is awake and up to nothing, no menu open and nobody leaving (`morph_step`, `Pet::morph_at`; `CHONKGENE_MORPH=<seconds>` for trying it).
  - **Her card** (settings, only where she can change): 「人型」 with 隨牠變 / 維持原型 / 維持人型 (a lock takes her into its form at once), 「髮型」 綁單馬尾 / 綁雙馬尾 / 公主頭, 「髮色」 dots (白色, 紅色, 金色, 夜色: the hair and its tips); the preview shows her as she is, in her look (`make_preview`). Hint 「牠偶爾會自己變身，也可以讓牠一直維持一種樣子。」 (牠, as §1.11 has it for every pet). English and Japanese written.
  - **Without a GPU**: the switch is not there and she stays on all fours; a saved human form waits for a machine with one.
  - **Checked under Xvfb** (`CHONKGENE_RENDERER=gpu`, the fox in 白色 with red twin tails and the Shiba): asked at 3.3 s, the ring and orbs of fox-fire rise where she stood and she is a girl 0.2 s later (her bake), walks about; back at 13.3 s, on all fours again 0.8 s later (the beast form's bake, longer), the fire covering the gap. Review `inbox/fox-morph.png` (the frames, her card, the menu).
  - **Trying it**: `CHONKGENE_STAGE=morph` (the fox 變身 from her menu at 3 s and back at 13 s, her frames dumped with `CHONKGENE_DUMP`); `settings_screenshots` has `settings-pets-fox*.png` (her card, tall; run with `CHONKGENE_RENDERER=gpu` on a software GPU to see the section).
  - Tests: `the_foxs_form_and_looks_are_saved`, `a_save_from_before_pinning_still_loads` (the new fields default).
  - **Not hers yet** (next, in the order above): sitting and sleeping are still the birds' (legs folded under); gestures move only what a bird has; touches, hugs and scenes take her as they find her; no lines of her own for changing (she says hello).
- **Sitting and sleeping, built (2026-11-24, `go on`)**: her own, in place of the birds' (legs folded under a body that hardly sank, so she floated with her legs sticking out). In `Waddle::child` (nothing on a bird), on top of the bird's pose:
  - **Sitting (女孩坐)**: on her heels, her legs folded back and out either side of her (`SEAT_LEGS` 78° back, `SEAT_SPLAY` 62° out), so her feet show beside her skirt; the skirt spread round her down to the floor (`SEAT_FLARE`: the `hips` bone, which the skirt hangs from, grown 20%); dropped 57% of her legs' length (`SEAT_LOW`); leaning back a little, hands in her lap. Tried first: legs straight out in front, the skirt laid over her lap; but from the app's camera (9° down, the picture's bottom at the floor line) legs lying along the floor in front of her are under the picture's bottom (behind the taskbar on the desktop), so she looked cut off at the waist; everything of this sit is above the floor line. Resting (趴下, a still mouse) and her moods (laughing, fed up, sulking) sit the same way.
  - **Asleep, two ways** (the fox's sleep weights, as her beast form's: 2 to 5): `Chin` dozes off sitting up (the sit, slumped forward 9°, head drooped); `Curl` curls up on her side (`curl`, eased in): rolled 90° onto her left side facing right (her right facing left), her legs drawn up (the upper one further), the lower arm up under her head as a pillow, the upper over her front, her head laid over; laid on the floor by the renderer (`ground`, as the beasts rolling over are), and her nine tails over her hips and legs like a quilt (`quilt`: each root aimed with `aim`, now `pub(crate)`, up over her upper side and toward you, spread from her hips down her legs in fan order, none near her face). Aimed up and back first, they stood over her like a fan; toward you, they read as a quilt. `Mover::sleep_pose` now reaches the birds' walker too (they still tuck in).
  - **Test**: `the_fox_child_sits_on_her_heels_and_curls_up_under_her_tails` (sitting: her lowest on the floor, her head over 0.25 lower, each foot out beside her and behind, none under the floor; curled: laid on the floor, lying along it, no tail under her body or over her face). Mutation-checked: legs out in front fails it, and so do tails without the quilt (they went 0.34 under her).
  - **Looking at it**: `examples/touch.rs` takes `fox-human` (`STYLE`, `HAIR`, `COAT=` by name) and has a `sit` scene. Review `inbox/fox-sit-sleep.png` (sitting, dozing and curled up in three looks; the sit close up; hugged asleep, laughing, fed up, sulking).
  - **Not hers yet**: gestures (stretch, yawn, wave…) still move only what a bird has; the dance, touches by zone and hugs take her as they find her (a hug asleep keeps her curled on the floor); her own lines for changing form.
- **Roger's look at her in the app (2026-11-24)**: 「九尾的人型與其他寵物比例差太多，且動作有點生疏」, and `inbox/chonkgene.log` for the stutter.
  - **Proportions**: as measured off the sheet she was a slim figure about 2.8 heads tall; beside the round pets (a head as big as the rest of them) she looked a different kind of thing, half as wide. `figure_blend.py` now makes everything as measured and reshapes it as it is written (`SHAPE`, the pivots and the head's parts with it): the head with its ears and hair grown 1.45 about the neck (hair hanging below the neck goes over to the body's reshaping as it falls, so it still ends at the sash), the body 1.28 wider and deeper, the legs 0.65 and the body 0.88 as tall (the feet kept), the arms 1.12 about the shoulder at the sheet's slant, the tails moved with their root. `FIGURE_SHAPE="<head> <wide> <legs> <body> <arms>"` tries others; `1 1 1 1 1` is the sheet's. Review `inbox/fox-human-proportions.png` (A the sheet's, B, C now, D, beside the others). **Owed**: Roger's pick (C chosen meanwhile; another is one constant and `build_figure.sh`). The twin tails grew with her head: big, chibi-like; to be judged with the rest.
  - **The shadow** leaves out tails, hair (`head` zone) and a child's arms however low they hang (`mesh::shadow_of`): shorter legs brought her outer tails, long hair and her hands (held out as she is made) below the line her legs reach to. The old guard in the scripts (tails kept above it) is gone.
  - **Elbows**: each arm is two bones now, `arm_*` and `fore_*` (pivot half way down the sleeve: the sleeve blends across the elbow, the cuff and the hand are the forearm's), 62 of 64 bones.
  - **Her arms go where her hands go** (`waddle/child.rs`, a module of the waddle; nothing on a bird): two-bone reaching (`Arm::reach`: the palm, 1.6 upper arms past the elbow, put at the place, the elbow toward a pole), places given in arm lengths from the shoulder (so they fit her whatever her proportions), on her head (moving with it), at her mouth or on the floor, blended in layers: at her sides swinging with her steps → in her lap sitting → pillow and over her front curled up → feelings → fidgets → gestures → holding something. Arms up go up and out in a V: straight up, her short arms put her hands inside her big head and her sleeve across her face.
  - **Her own gestures** (the bird's did nothing without wings, or bowed her to peck at a snack): stretch (both arms up on her toes), yawn behind her hand (it follows her head thrown back), wave, 握手, a snack held in both hands and nibbled (the snack is drawn between her hands, not on the floor), kneeling to dig or to pick something up, a shy scratch at the side of her head (the fox's scratch), combing her side lock through her fingers (the fox's wash), a shake of the head that flings her hair, a little dance with her hands under her chin, a cheer for each hop, a twirl arms out, typing along, a finger to her cheek with a tilt, hands to her cheeks (rub), arms out for balance swinging her tails (swish) or kicking a ball (nudge), her own 肉肉舞 moves (hands up, on her hips, one up then the other, 「登登！」 one up and one on her hip), and hugs with her arms (round the other, over its shoulder, pinned and paddling in a bear hug, holding your thumb).
  - **Her answers to a touch**: laughing she sits rocking with her hands on her tummy (no more kicking a bird's legs), fed up her arms hang limp, sulking she folds them, shy her hands go to her cheeks, startled to her chest, blissful or patted together under her chin; a hand of hers touched comes up to yours.
  - **Her own fidgets** (kit `child`, not the four-legged fox's licks and paw shuffles): new `HairTuck` (her hair behind her ear), `HandsBehind` (rocking on her heels), `ArmSwing`, `Fiddle`; and a tilt of the head, weight from foot to foot, her tails' ripple, a little shake that rings her bell; asleep, a twitch of the hand under her cheek.
  - **Carrying in her hands**: `Renderer::carry_at` (between her hands for her, the mouth for everyone else) for what she carries, walks to and lets fall; `set_holding` (every frame) holds it to her chest. `mouth()` stays the mouth (the beach's sand heap, effects).
  - **Tests**: `her_palm_lands_where_she_reaches` (either arm, places all round, the posed palm within 0.01), `her_hands_go_where_her_gestures_put_them` (yawn over her mouth as posed, a snack under it, a stretch up and clear of her head, a scratch on it, the lap sitting; mutation-checked: a mouth that does not follow the head and arms straight up both fail it).
  - **Looking at it**: the `mesh` example's `life`/`smile`/`scene` take `fox-human`; review `inbox/fox-human-moves.png`.
  - **The log** (`inbox/chonkgene.log`): the same run as the one of 2026-11-20 (the same 3688/4640/1499 ms stalls, and its frames lines have no `late` count, added with the fix b48a6c0 that day), so it is from a build before that fix. What it does show besides: with seven pets the GPU (UHD 770) was over its 25% budget (28–32%) and each walking pet was shown at 15–20 frames a second of 40–55 asked, held back by the budget. The log's first line now names the commit it was built from (`build.rs`, `CHONKGENE_COMMIT`). **Owed**: a log from the newest build; if walking still looks choppy with many pets at the budget, cheaper frames are next (the beast meshes are 34 000–68 000 vertices, simplified as hers are, or fewer pixels).
- **Roger's second look (2026-11-25, `go on`)**: proportions **C** (chosen); 「九尾人形還是和預計有點差距」 after `…人獸型(小)-3D全型.jpeg`: 「畢竟是女九尾，給點上圍。尾巴可以沿用獸型態」; 「平時、施法時的咒印，還是和預計有點差距」; the moves 「看起來還可以」; the log sent was again the old one.
  - **What she is**: a fox spirit, a young woman drawn chibi, not a child: the code and these notes no longer call her one (`waddle/child.rs` → `waddle/human.rs`, `Waddle::human_form`, fidget kit `human`, tests `the_fox_human_form_…`). Her touch zones stay head, cheeks, hands and tails.
  - **Her figure**: the robe rounded out softly over her chest (`figure_blend.py` `bust`: two soft swells running together in the middle, between the collar and the sash, 15 sheet pixels at the most), as the 3D全型 sheet's side view has it; modest, clothed, hardly showing from the side under her hair.
  - **Her tails are the beast form's**: the same plumes (`gen_tails.PRESETS["quad"]`: 1.9 long, as fat, fanned 112°), from her own root; 紅髮版 (白色) takes the beast's colours too (white, a pink blush, red tips: `gen_human.py`), the other coats already had them.
  - **Her seal** (`paint.wgsl`/`face.rs` `seal_marks`, placed from her eyes): at rest, as the 平常 picture: three leaves between the upper halves of her eyes (the middle one tallest) and three fine strokes on each cheek fanned like whiskers; casting (`g`), as the 拆解圖2 faces: the middle leaf grows into a tall flame drop with a hollow, wings curl up out of its foot, the cheek strokes grow longer and bolder, a flick of eyeliner at each eye's corner, all in the mark's colour; no rings round her eyes (the beast's seal keeps them), and her eyes keep their deep colour, only a little lit. The cheek strokes reach her cheeks and chin because a face's paint now reaches as far as the model says (`face.paint`, 0.05 unless given; hers 0.16: her cheeks and chin are parts of their own, standing out further).
  - **A painting bug, every fox**: a leaf's width was `pow(sin(π h), 0.7)`, and past its ends sin(π h) is a hair under 0, so NaN; on the CPU `min` dropped it, on some GPUs (llvmpipe here) the whole half plane past a leaf's tip took the mark's colour (on her a band of it under her fringe, and before that the "brow leaves"). Clamped in both painters; the fresnel's `pow` got the same guard.
  - **Review**: `inbox/fox-human-look.png` (both looks, front, three quarters, back, the beast beside), `inbox/fox-human-seal.png` (at rest and casting beside Roger's pictures). **Owed**: his look; the casting crest is smaller than the picture's (her fringe comes down to her eyes); in 施法狀態 her hair also turns gold to red and her tails burn (the tails do already), not done.
  - **The log** was the same old file again (239 734 bytes, no commit on its first line): the newest build writes `chonkGene 0.6.0-beta.1 (<commit>) on windows` first, in `chonkGene-data\chonkgene.log` beside the exe if that folder is there, else `%APPDATA%\chonkGene\chonkgene.log`, started afresh each run.

### Roger's twenty-fifth pass (2026-11-19)

Asked how best to handle 寵物上場順序 (who is out on the desktop when there are more pets than it holds), I proposed (1) an order you set, (2) swapping instead of a silently higher limit, (3) taking turns. Roger: 「可以，另外也可以新增隨機輪班 預設關」, and 「摸摸頭，沒有手出現，動作上也需再強化一下」.

What it was before: the order was the adoption order and could not be changed; one adopted with the desktop full went straight to 寵物窩 unseen; calling one out by hand with the desktop full raised the limit by one (more GPU load, unasked). With eleven new species planned (§8 "New species") and seven places, later ones would never come out.

1. **上場順序** (built 2026-11-19): the order of 「我的寵物」's names is the order they come out in and the reverse of the order they rest in; the pet picked has ‹ › in its name to move it earlier or later (`Pets::shift`, `Msg::Order` with the ids, `Pets::reorder` in the pet process). The hint under the names: 「按 ‹ › 調整順序，排越前面越先上桌面。」. Resting pets' names are faded (only the dot and name, not the border, so the one picked still shows).
2. **換班, not a higher limit** (built 2026-11-19): one called out by hand (`Msg::CallOut`) or just adopted (`Pets::adopt`) with the desktop full comes out, and the last one out in the order that is not 常駐 goes to rest in its place (`Pets::call_out` → `Room`); only if every other one out is 常駐 does the limit grow, as before. Both processes run the same function on the same list.
   - **On the desktop** (`sync_desktop`): a pet gone to rest (換班, 輪班, a lower limit, switched off by hand) stops, waves and says a new line, `to_den` (「換你囉！我去窩裡睡一下～」, 「……換你。我去睡了。」, 「換你上，姊姊歇會兒。」; every species, three languages), and its window goes 2.2 s later (`LEAVE_SECS`; not while you hold it). The one coming out waits for it and appears where it stood, in a puff of dust and sparkles, and says hello at once (`App::handover`). One released, or any while they are put away, goes at once.
   - **常駐** (「一直待在桌面上」, hint 「換班時不會換到牠。」, `PetSave::pinned`, `#[serde(default)]`): never the one swapped out or sent to rest by 輪班; a lower limit rests it last. The switch shows under 「在桌面上」 only when there are more pets than the desktop holds.
   - Found on the way: `PetSave` had no serde default, so a save without the new field was set aside as `pets.ron.bad` (every pet gone). Test `a_save_from_before_pinning_still_loads`. Also 「關掉就在寵物窩休息。」 (130 px at 13 px) broke 休/息 in its 240 px card; the switch rows' gap is 8 px now.
3. **隨機輪班** (built 2026-11-19, **off by default**): 一般 › 「隨機輪班」 (hint 「每隔一段時間，隨機和寵物窩裡的換班。」): 不輪班 / 30 分鐘 / 1 小時 / 3 小時 (`Settings::rotate_minutes`, `ROTATE_CHOICES`). About that often (a fifth either way) one pet out that is free to go (not 常駐, not held, hugged, patted, in a skit or up to something, on its feet or asleep) changes places with one resting, both at random (`Pets::pick_shift`, `Pets::swap`, `App::rotate_step`); with nobody free or resting, or while they are put away, a menu is open or one is saying goodbye, it tries again in a minute. Saved at once; the settings window reads who is out back from the file (not within 2 s of a change of its own, `DESK_SETTLE`). `CHONKGENE_SHIFT=<seconds>` makes the round short, for trying it.
4. **摸摸頭 with a hand** (built 2026-11-19): from the menu your hand comes down onto its head (the hug's see-through child's hand, 2.1× its size so it covers the top of the head, laid flat with the fingers toward the back of its head so the face stays clear; fingers pointing down over the head covered the Fold's eyes), pats three times (each time it is pressed in under the hand, squishes and squints, and pops back), then strokes it to the back of its head and forward twice (it leans into the hand, eyes shut, the dent following), and goes; then the happy hop and hearts (`props::PatKind`, `pat_pose`, `pat_hand`, `draw_pat_hand`; `Pet::pat_you`, `Pet::pat_step` driving `Animator::press`/`knead`/`poke` on the head, as a finger would). What it says comes as the stroking starts. Asleep it is only stroked, slowly, and sleeps on; in a mood (有起床氣) the hand comes down, it flinches and says 「（躲開）」, the hand goes. Without the hand (being held, in a skit, in the air, up to something, already in your hands, going to rest) it is pleased at once as before, and the log says why. Tests `a_pat_comes_down_three_times_then_strokes`, `the_patting_hand_sits_on_the_crown_and_keeps_off_the_face`; `examples/touch.rs pat`; `CHONKGENE_STAGE=pat`.
- **Checked under Xvfb** (`CHONKGENE_RENDERER=gpu`, five pets, three places, 阿柴 常駐, `CHONKGENE_SHIFT=7`): four shifts in 34 s, 阿柴 never sent; the duckling said 「……換你了，呱。」 and waved, went, and the owl appeared where it stood with 「咕咕，主人好。」; 摸摸頭 on the walking Shiba: it sat, the hand followed its head through the pats and strokes, then the hop. Settings screenshots in three languages (`settings_screenshots` now has an eighth pet resting, so 「一直待在桌面上」 shows).
- **Owed**: Roger's look on Windows (the hand's size and place on each species; whether 2.2 s of goodbye is right); a walk off the screen's edge instead of popping out, if he wants more.

#### Roger on the above (2026-11-19, same day)

1. 「摸摸頭的手不是按著寵物的頭」. In the app the hand sat beside and above the head, fingers sideways, only the wrist touching; and the pet looked up at the touch (the gaze toward `Touch::at`), bringing its face under the hand. **Changed**: the hand is laid over the top of the head from above, the back of the hand toward you, fingers down onto the crown and tipped toward the back of the head, their tips short of the eyes, the palm and wrist above (2.4× the hug's hand; `pat_hand`, `pat_angle`); down, it sinks into the dent. Patted, the pet no longer turns to look at the hand (`Animator::patted` → `Touch::from_above`, no gaze in `MeshRenderer::feel`): it faces you, squints, its head squashed at each pat. A palm-down hand seen from the side was tried first and read as a ribbon floating over the head. Review `inbox/pat-hand.png` (seven species), `inbox/pat-in-app.png` (Xvfb).
2. 「不要用方向鍵換位置，改用拖曳。上方多一個出場 card，把要出場的寵物拖曳到上面。再來點兩下變為常駐，就不會受到隨機輪班影響」. **Changed**: 「我的寵物」 has two cards, **「出場中」** (`{} / {} 隻` against the limit) and **「在寵物窩休息」**; a name is dragged (Slint 1.18 `DragArea`/`DropArea`, in-window on every backend; under the cursor a drawn copy of the name, `chip_image`) onto another name (before it, or after it on its right half, a bar showing where) or onto a card (last). Into a full 「出場中」, the last one out not 常駐 goes to rest, first in line in the den (`Pets::seat`). A double-click on a name in 「出場中」 makes it 常駐 (a 「常駐」 tag on it) or not. The 「在桌面上」 and 「一直待在桌面上」 switches and the ‹ › are gone. The order kept is now those out first, then those resting (`Pets::normalize`): a lower limit rests the last out, who come back first; 隨機輪班's newcomer takes the place of the one gone (`Pets::swap`). The settings window sends who is where as `Msg::Roster` (order, out, 常駐; `Pets::take_roster`); `Msg::Pet` no longer carries out or 常駐 (an edit sent just after 隨機輪班 would undo it).
   - Found by the test: picking a pet rebuilt the lists of names, and Slint counts a click on a new element as a first click, so a double-click on a name not yet picked never made it 常駐. The rows are now updated in place when the same pets are in the same places (`show_rows`). Test `dragging_names_moves_pets_and_a_double_click_pins` drives real pointer events on the window: a drag from the den onto a name out, then a double-click.
   - Also found: `seat` put a pet dropped on itself last.
3. 「說再見的按鈕一樣放在右上方（領養新寵物）旁邊會不會比較好操作」: yes; 「說再見…」 is in the title row beside 「＋ 領養新寵物」, and asking first takes that row (「確定要和{}說再見嗎？」, 再想想 / 說再見). The pet's column has only its picture now.
- **Owed**: Roger's look on Windows (dragging with a real mouse; the hand on each pet).

#### The name boxes (Roger, 2026-11-19, same day)

「名字和牠怎麼叫你的輸入框還是沒有改善」: asked what, he answered the look does not match, finishing an entry (stored when you leave the box, closing) is not done well, and the length should be held to 12 or fewer. Before: the standard `LineEdit` (Fluent, square, small type), stored at every key (an emptied box showed empty while the old name stayed), no end to an entry (Enter did nothing, a click elsewhere left the cursor in the box), and as many characters as typed went in, cut to 12 (8) only when stored. **Changed** (`CuteField` in settings.slint, `commit_text`/`commit_fields` in settings.rs):
- A rounded white box like the names' chips, a peach ring while typing, 15 px type; while typing the count on its right (「3/12」, orange at the limit); the text slides under a clip to keep the cursor in sight. Both boxes one width, sharing the row (160 px each as before).
- No more than 12 characters go into the name, 8 into 「牠怎麼叫你」 (`MAX_NAME`, `MAX_YOU`; IME composition is not cut, only what it commits).
- Stored when typing ends: Enter, a click on the window's background (the focus goes to a `FocusScope` there), another pet picked, adopting begun, another page, the window closed; then 「已儲存」 shows in the box a moment. Trimmed; empty or unchanged, the stored one comes back into the box. Esc drops what was typed.
- Test `a_name_is_stored_when_typing_ends_and_kept_short` types on the window (13 characters → 12, nothing stored until Enter, an emptied box left by a click put back, Esc). Review `inbox/name-boxes.png` (typing, three languages).

### Roger's twenty-sixth pass (feedback only, 2026-11-20; to do next)

Roger's 「20261119 回饋報告」, on a build from before `b59956f` (the name boxes) as far as can be told:

1. **「右鍵選單太亂，請統合整理一下。另外如 大家的大小、改名字…等直接進介面修改。」** Now the first page has up to 16 rows (`App::open_menu`, app.rs: 抱抱…, 摸摸頭, 餵零食, 改名字…, 幫牠換造型…, 讓牠去睡覺, 叫牠…, 施法… (fox), 玩躲貓貓, 場景…, 一起玩…, 大家的大小…, 說再見…, 全部收起來, 設定…, 結束). 改名字… and 幫牠換造型… already open the settings window (`Action::Edit`); 大家的大小… is a page of its own. **Proposed grouping, to show Roger as a picture before building**: the first page about ten rows in three groups: (a) 抱抱…, 摸摸頭, 餵零食, 讓牠去睡覺 / 輕輕叫醒牠; (b) 叫牠… (the commands, 施法 as a section of it for the fox), 一起玩… (the toys, 玩躲貓貓, and the scenes as a section, so 場景… goes); (c) 設定… opening the settings on this pet (改名字…, 幫牠換造型…, 說再見… all live there now), 全部收起來, 結束肉肉賴桌. 大家的大小 goes into the settings (一般 › 大家的大小), and so on for anything else that is a setting. 改名字 from the settings opened this way: the name box focused, its text selected, ready to type over. The tray's menu to match.
   **Picture shown to Roger (2026-11-20, `inbox/menu-proposal.png`, drawn by the real menu renderer; waiting for his word before building)**: first page 9 rows in three groups (抱抱…, 摸摸頭, 餵零食, 讓牠去睡覺 / 叫牠…, 一起玩… / 設定… with the hint 「名字・造型」, 全部收起來, 結束肉肉賴桌); 叫牠… = the commands, then a section captioned 「施法」 for the fox (12 rows with 返回); 一起玩… = 玩躲貓貓, a 「場景」 section (its own scenes, then the shared ones; the fox's 狐火 only under 施法, as `Magic::Foxfire` and `CallScene(Foxfire)` both start the same scene), a 「大家一起玩」 section with the five toys. Captions are `Item::caption` in menu.rs (a taller gap with a heading on the separator line). Below the menus: the settings window's 寵物 page (name box, 說再見…) and 一般 › 大家的大小, both already there, so the menu only drops rows. Tray proposed: 收起來／叫回來, 一起玩 › (the five toys, 聚光燈 added), 場景 ›, 設定…, 結束肉肉賴桌 (大家的大小 › removed). The same screenshot shows the name box (`CuteField`, `b59956f`) with 阿柴 in it, for item 3.
   **Built 2026-11-20** (Roger: 「go on」 on the picture): as pictured. `menu_page(page, &MenuFacts)` in app.rs builds every page from plain facts (`App::menu_facts` gathers them; `SceneRows` for the 場景 section), tested by actions and captions; pages `Spells`, `Sizes`, `Scenes` and actions `Spells`, `Sizes`, `SizeAll`, `Scenes`, `Bye`, `Settings` are gone (goodbye asks in the settings window; its two-click row in the menu went with it). 設定… is `Action::Edit`: the settings open on the 我的寵物 tab with the name box focused and its text selected (`ask_for_name`: `tab` 0 + `name-focus`, two-way to `CuteField.focus-ask`, which takes the focus on `init` or on change and clears itself), also when the window is already open (`show <id>`). Tray: `LABELS` 收起來／叫回來, 設定…, 結束; submenus 一起玩 (`PLAY`, with `TrayCommand::Stage` → `App::tray_stage`: beside the free pet nearest the middle) and 場景, a separator after them; `TrayCommand::Size` and `tray::SIZES` removed. Look tool: `cargo test -p chonkgene -- --ignored menu_screenshots` → target/screens/menu-*.png.
2. **「部份的動物的行為沒有連貫邏輯，如柴犬骨頭出現在地面上時，嘴巴沒去咬下一秒卻在嘴裡」.** The bone scene (`Scene::Bone`, app.rs ~2679/2703; `props::carried`): the bone jumps from the floor into the mouth. To do: walk to it, lower the head to it (a sniff, the mouth at the bone), the bone carried up into the mouth over a few frames from where it lay, then the chewing. Then go through the other pick-ups and hand-overs for the same jump (the ball and frisbee brought back, seeds pecked, the butterfly, toys taken from the cursor) and fix any found.
3. **「改名字的介面除了看不到名字，還不和目前的風格不符」.** 改名字… opens the settings window on the pet; the boxes were the standard `LineEdit` until `b59956f` (CuteField, the window's own look). Ask Roger to look again on `b59956f`; if the name still cannot be seen, find out where (the box empty? its text the colour of the box, e.g. Windows' dark mode on the standard widgets? the box out of sight when the window opens?).
4. **「寵物們從高空飛下來卡頓嚴重」.** A fall already counts as moving for the frame rate (`Pet::travelling` includes `Motion::Fall`). Suspects: the window moved every frame on Windows (`SetWindowPos` cost, presenting while moving), the shared GPU budget when several fall at once (all seven at start-up, or 全部叫回來), the switch into the glide pose. Ask Roger for `chonkgene.log` (it has the `frames:` lines) after a few falls from high, and reproduce under Xvfb with `CHONKGENE_DUMP` timings.

   **Item 2 built 2026-11-20**: a pick-up in app/play.rs (`stoop` → `picking` → `Picking::{Busy,Got,Lost}`; `Fun.pickup`, `Fun.lift`): stopped by the thing, the new gesture `Act::Pick` (core `PICK_SECS` 1.0, `PICK_GRAB` 0.4; skin.rs/waddle.rs `PICK_BODY`/`PICK_HEAD`/`PICK_LEGS`, side-on `WALK_YAW` while it runs, since turned toward you at `REST_YAW` the mouth drew back ~70 px from the bone) brings the mouth to the floor (test `picking_up_brings_the_mouth_down_to_the_floor`: every species within 0.2 of it); at `PICK_GRAB` the carrier is set and `props::lifted` draws it rising from where it lay into `props::held_at` over `LIFT_SECS` 0.25. The bone scene walks to `mouth_over` the bone, picks it up, then lies down to chew. The audit: fetch's floor grabs (the Shiba's frisbee, the owl's ball) now stoop too (claimed meanwhile); things snapped up in the air glide in by the same lift; every let-go (`Order::Drop`, `Order::Done`, `tickled_loose`, the scene's end or interruption) goes through `drop_from_mouth`, falling from the mouth instead of appearing at the feet; the bone left at the end of its scene lies `LEFT_LYING` 6 s (it vanished from the mouth). Seeds, the butterfly and the cursor's toys carry nothing, so nothing jumped there. Checked under Xvfb with `CHONKGENE_STAGE=bone` + `CHONKGENE_DUMP` (dumps now also while carrying or in the bone scene, and the carried things' windows).

5. **Added to the same report (2026-11-20): 「若先開啟設定，此時在右下的資料夾 點選 `結束肉肉賴桌`，toolbar 上的設定介面不會關掉」.** The settings window is a child process; quitting the pets left it running. **Fixed 2026-11-20**: `App::close_settings` from `exiting()` (every way out) writes `quit` on the child's stdin (kills it only if that fails); settings.rs `asked()` reads `Asked::{Show, Quit}`, and `Quit` ends its event loop, so `run()` still keeps what was being typed (sent, or saved straight to disk once the pets are gone). Checked under Xvfb with `CHONKGENE_STAGE=settings-quit` (settings open at 3 s, quit at 8 s, no settings process left by 12 s); the tray itself cannot be clicked there (no StatusNotifierWatcher), but the tray's 結束 and the menu's go the same way.

**Suggested order**: 1 (the menu: picture first, then build), 3 (a look on `b59956f`, small), 2 (the bone, then the other pick-ups), 4 (with his log).

### Roger's bug report (2026-11-20, with `inbox/chonkgene.log`)

1. **「牠怎麼叫你」 改了以後，動物台詞還是沒變.** The change does reach the pet process (the log shows the same `Msg::Pet` renaming 小九 to 小九九 at 761 s) and `say` fills `{you}` from it at once; but only 33 lines used `{you}` (hello, welcome back, waking, levelling up, a few of the Shiba's), all rare. **Fixed 2026-11-20**: a new line `CalledYou` (`called_you`), said by that pet as soon as its 「牠怎麼叫你」 changes (`App` on `Msg::Pet`, unless asleep); one more `{you}` line each in `pat`, `eat` and `hugged` for every species, in zh-TW, ja and en (`lines.rs` tests: every line written, and fitting a bubble with the longest names).
2. **九尾三種抱下方尾巴會被邊框遮住.** Reproduced with the touch example: in 環抱, 舉高高 and 搖搖抱 her tails hung past her picture's bottom (a flat cut 49 to 147 px wide); a test over every species found more: the hind feet in 舉高高 (the Shiba and cats), a sleeping chick or duckling cradled. Causes: her tails wrapped round to the front tipped down; the carry pose was taller than the picture; birds left out the hug's pose whenever sitting or asleep (`Act::Hug` not `seated`); and the renderer settles a rolled-over or sleeping pet's lowest skin onto its floor, which undid any lift in the rig. **Fixed 2026-11-20**: `MeshRenderer::settle` keeps a pet in any hug from going below its floor (held by you, `Hug::ByYou`, at least `HELD_CLEAR` above it), lifting it as far as needed (one rule for every species and hold); her tails stay at her middle when wrapped round you (`HELD_TAILS`) and curl up when she is held upright (`CARRY_TAILS`); 舉高高 tips the body up 22° and lets the hind legs hang 22° (`CARRY_PITCH`, `CARRY_DANGLE`, from 48° and 50°) so feet to ears it fits; birds have the hug sitting too. Test `a_pet_held_in_your_arms_is_never_cut_off` (tests/mesh.rs): held in each way, awake and asleep, no pet comes within 3 px of the left, top or right edge, and along the bottom it touches no more than standing on its feet.
2b. **Reported again the same day** (「九尾 抱抱下方尾巴會被邊框裁掉」): the holds were fixed, but not the hugs between pets. A second test, `a_pet_hugging_another_is_never_cut_off` (cheek to cheek either side, the fox's bear hug, held in it), found her bear hug worst (154 px cut: up on her hind legs her tails swept down), those she squeezes (53–112 px), and paws past the floor cheek to cheek (13–35 px). **Fixed**: the floor rule above now covers every hug; in her bear hug her tails tip back up (`BEAR_TAILS`). Checked in the app under Xvfb with the fox first (`CHONKGENE_STAGE` wrap, carry, cradle and the new `hug-other`, her frames dumped): nothing of her along the bottom row in any of them (7 px as she is put down after the cradle, as standing).
3. **Found on the way**: `settings::tests::dragging_names_moves_pets_and_a_double_click_pins` failed in a `--release` build (at `7caf11d` too), and once in a busy debug run. **Fixed 2026-11-20**: its pointer positions were taken with the 「沒有在執行」 notice showing on top, and whether that notice was there yet depended on how fast the test ran; the headless platform now has a clock the tests move on (`pass`, thread-local `CLOCK`, `Platform::duration_since_start`) instead of sleeping, and the drag test lets the notice settle first. Both builds pass every run.
4. **Twenty-sixth pass, item 4 (falls stutter), from this log (2026-11-20, `go on`)**. The log has three stalls where the whole program stopped: 「held up: woke 3688 / 4640 / 1499 ms late」 (8 s, 31 s, 35 s), the second during the Shiba's fall from far above the screen (the frisbee fell from its mouth at y −4816). A moving pet's frame (walking, flying, falling: `keep_up`) still on the GPU was waited for on the app's thread with no limit (`PollType::wait_indefinitely`, which also waits for every frame queued after it, then up to `MAP_LANDS` 2 s more for the map), so one frame the GPU was slow over (seconds, on his Intel UHD 770; a 4.5 s 「waiting gpu」 was seen on 2026-11-11 and never found) froze every pet and window. **Changed**: `gpu::Wait { No, Upto(d), Done }`; `MeshCanvas` keeps its frame's `SubmissionIndex` and waits for that one alone; a moving pet waits at most `KEEP_UP_WAIT` 12 ms, then the frame stays on its way and shows next time (test `a_frame_waited_for_briefly_stays_on_its_way_and_is_taken_later`); only tests and the eager renderer wait to the end. The log's `frames:` lines now say `late N` (waits cut short). Not reproducible here (llvmpipe never stalls): under Xvfb with seven pets on `CHONKGENE_STAGE=move`, the same frames shown (the software GPU is the limit) and the loop stepping a little more often (the birds 37 → 45 a second); Roger's next log should show whether 「held up」 is gone.
5. **The log** also shows the GPU at 28–46% of the time (budget 25%) on his Intel UHD 770, each pet shown 15–20 frames a second of 40–55 asked: for item 4 of the twenty-sixth pass (falls stutter), still to look into.

### Roger, 2026-11-20: 隨機輪班 too slow

「隨機輪班時間都太久了，可以 測試按鈕 和 15分鐘嗎」. **Built**: `ROTATE_CHOICES` 0, 15, 30, 60, 180 (「15 分鐘」 added to the segmented choice; a stored value shows as the nearest, test `the_rotation_interval_shows_as_its_choice`); under it 「現在換一次」 (いま交代する / Swap one now), off when 寵物窩 is empty: the settings send `Msg::ShiftNow`, the pet process swaps one free pet out for one resting at once (`App::shift_now`, shared with `rotate_step`), whether 隨機輪班 is on or not, and the next round counts from then. Checked under Xvfb by sending `ShiftNow` on the socket to a running app with eight pets: the log said who changed places at once.

### Roger, 2026-11-23: 「九尾 抱抱下方尾巴會被邊框裁掉」, a third time

On the build with `faba221` (CI built `efd544d` and `1edbbf2` green), so not the two cases fixed on 2026-11-20. Under Xvfb with the fox first (`CHONKGENE_STAGE` hug-other, wrap, carry, cradle, hug-asleep) nothing of her reached the bottom of her picture, at her default chonk 0.33. A sweep of the fox over chonk 0, 0.33, 0.66 and 1, at 100% and 150%, facing either way, through every hug and 1.5 s after, found it: **as a hug ends**. `MeshRenderer::settle` lifted a pet in a hug only while `Act::Hug` lasted; the moment it ended the lift was gone, but her pose (the tails' springs) took about half a second to come back, so she dropped and her lower tails were cut flat by the bottom of the picture: one frame (8 px) at chonk 0.33, half a second (up to 45 px wide at 100%, 65 px at 150%) for a chubby fox, after a bear hug, cheek to cheek or held by you. The 2026-11-20 tests left out the last 0.3 s of the hug ("put down again after") and used chonk 0.33 only.

**Fixed**: the floor of a hug (`hug_floor`: `FLOOR`, held by you `+ HELD_CLEAR`) holds after the hug too. For `HUG_HOLD` 1 s the most it may lift her comes down no faster than her pose needs (and at most as an exponential fall, `HUG_LAND` 0.15 s), then whether or not, so she comes down with her pose, gently, and always lands; a chubby fox hovers a few pixels for that second, then sits down. A time-only fall was tried first: the stricter tests then caught a cradled Shiba rolling back up off its back slower than that (one frame, 15 px). **Found on the way and fixed**: the renderer timed every hug's pose by `gesture_secs(Act::Hug)`, 3.2 s, so in the 9 s 搖搖抱 her tails draped over your hands and the rocking went after 3.2 s while your hands stayed; it now times a hug by `Hug::secs`. Tests `a_pet_held_in_your_arms_is_never_cut_off` and `a_pet_hugging_another_is_never_cut_off` now share `hug_edges`: every species at chonk 0.33 and 1, through the whole hug and 0.6 s after, may touch the bottom no more than on its feet before or settled after (+4 px), and never come within 3 px of the left, top or right edge. Checked in the app under Xvfb with a chonk 1 fox first (`CHONKGENE_STAGE` hug-other and cradle, her frames dumped): nothing of her along the bottom row in the bear hug, the cradle, or the 1.5 s after either (lying asleep after the cradle, as lying always does).

### Roger's twenty-seventh pass (feedback only, 2026-11-25; to do next)

Roger's 「20261125 回饋報告」, talked through with him the same day (his answers folded in).

1. **「`她怎麼叫你` 可以多一個統一窗口設定」: one 「牠怎麼叫你」 for every pet.** Plan, two layers:
   - In 一般, a box **「大家怎麼叫你」**, empty by default. Empty, every pet keeps its species' default (主人, 奴才, 同學… have character; one value for all should not wipe that out unasked).
   - Filled, every pet whose own 「牠怎麼叫你」 nobody changed (still at its species' default in any language, as `store.rs` already tells) uses it, **and so does every pet adopted after**. A pet's own box, filled, still wins; empty, it shows the shared value greyed and follows it.
   - Changed, each pet that follows says `called_you`, a few seconds apart (not seven bubbles at once).
   - Better than an 「套用到全部」 button, which works once and misses the pets adopted later.

   **Built 2026-11-25** (`go on`):
   - **Stored**: `Settings::you` (empty by default, cut to `MAX_YOU`, `Settings::sanitise` on load and on `Msg::Settings`).
   - **Who follows**: `PetSave::calls_you(everyone)` gives the shared one when it is set and the pet's own is untouched (`you_untouched`: its species' default in any language, and not `own_you`). `own_you` (new, `serde(default)`) marks a 「牠怎麼叫你」 typed in by hand that reads as the default: asked to keep 「主人」 while the shared one is 「阿傑」, the Shiba keeps 「主人」. Typed with nothing shared, the default is just the default.
   - **The settings window**: 一般 has 「大家怎麼叫你」 (a `CuteField`; hint 「留空的話，牠們各叫各的。」 / 「沒有另外改過的寵物，都會這樣叫你。」). A pet's box is empty while it follows, the shared name greyed in it (`CuteField.placeholder`); its hint reads 「留空的話，就照「大家怎麼叫你」。」. Emptied, or typed as the shared one, it follows again (`follow_everyone`). Esc in any `CuteField` now puts back what the box held when typing began (it emptied the box and relied on an empty entry being put back, which an empty 「牠怎麼叫你」 no longer is). Leaving the page while typing stores the shared box too (`commit_fields`).
   - **The pets**: `say` fills `{you}` from `calls_you` (`Ctx::everyone_you`). The shared one changed, each pet out whose name for you changed (`told_anew`) says `called_you`, the first after 0.3 s and the rest 1.2–2 s apart (`App::called`, `called_step`; not asleep; dropped while put away). A pet's own changed in the settings says it only if what it calls you really changed.
   - **Words**: ja and en in `tools/i18n/*.txt`.
   - **Tests**: `everyone_calls_you_the_same_unless_one_was_told_otherwise` (store), `a_shared_name_is_tried_out_by_the_pets_that_take_it` (app), `one_name_for_everyone_and_a_pets_own_still_wins` (settings). Screenshots `settings-general-you*.png`, `settings-pets-you*.png` (`settings_screenshots`).
2. **Actions by zone, by tapping** (「互動改善建議 和 擁抱的觸發機制更改」). A setting **「動作要點幾下」: 一下 / 兩下, default 兩下**. It replaces today's double-click anywhere = hug (`DOUBLE_CLICK`).

   | Zone | Action | Built already? |
   |---|---|---|
   | 頭 head | 摸摸頭 (the menu's routine) | yes |
   | 下巴 chin | a chin scratch of the species' own (table below) | new |
   | 手 front paw (birds: wing) | 握手 (a bird's wing: a high five) | yes, `叫牠… › 握手` |
   | 腳 hind paw (birds: foot) | 舉高高 | yes, `HugHold::Carry` |
   | 肩 shoulder | 環抱 | yes, `HugHold::Wrap` |
   | 背 back | 搖搖抱 | yes, `HugHold::Cradle` |
   | 肚子 belly, 臉頰 cheek | no action: the flesh dents in and springs back out, 「ㄉㄨㄞ ㄉㄨㄞ」 | the dent is there |
   | 肚子 while lying on its back | 摸肚肚, a belly rub | new |

   - **Two taps** (the default): the first tap is a poke as now, but small, and the turn to face you waits until the double-click time has passed (so a poke does not feel 0.4 s late). The zone is **locked at the first tap**: the pet moves between the taps, and the second may land elsewhere. The second tap counts if it lands on the same pet within the system's double-click time (Windows `GetDoubleClickTime`, else 0.4 s).
   - **One tap**: a tap on a zone with an action starts it. Pokes and the tickle stages stay on belly and cheeks only (Roger, 2026-11-25: 可以; the belly was always the main event, §1.2).
   - **Both modes**: bottom and tail are not in Roger's list and stay poke only. A long press is unchanged: a pat by fondness, a dislike, the 曼赤肯's trap.
   - **握手 against the fondness table** (every species dislikes a held paw): two taps offer your hand and it gives its paw gladly, while holding the paw down is still disliked. With a grudge, or asleep, it pulls the paw back.
   - **「ㄉㄨㄞ ㄉㄨㄞ」**: the press dent (`mesh.wgsl` `press`, negative depth springing back) springs past the surface into a bulge and wobbles two or three times before it settles, more on a chubbier pet. The Shiba's double chin needs a soft spot of its own.
   - **摸肚肚** when it lies on its back (the tickle's belly-up laugh, a belly-up sleep, after a 搖搖抱): the Shiba's hind leg kicks (the scratch reflex), the 曼赤肯's trap comes after 3 s as now, the 折耳貓 melts flat, the fox's tails sway. New idle to go with it, **翻肚討摸**: now and then, with the cursor near, a dog, cat or beast-form fox rolls over to show its belly, asking. Without it a rub is hard to find.
   - **New zones from the bones**, as `back` and `wing` were (`mesh::refine_zone`): `Shoulder` (the top of the body between the neck and the front legs; a bird's wing root), and `Paw` split into front and hind (a bird's wing is its hand, its foot its foot). A picture of every pet coloured by zone (`examples/zones.rs`) goes to Roger first, checked at the smallest size, where shoulder, back and chin are a few pixels.
   - **Showing what can be done**: over each zone the hand cursor changes pose (an open palm on the head, knuckles up under the chin, …). See also item 4.
   - **Chin scratches** (Roger gave the cats and the dog; the rest are mine to plan):

     | Species | Chin scratch |
     |---|---|
     | 柴犬 | the double chin held from below, patted and squeezed, the flesh bouncing 「ㄉㄨㄞ ㄉㄨㄞ」; eyes squeezed into a Shiba smile, the curled tail going |
     | 曼赤肯貓 | 反手撓下巴 (the back of the hand, knuckles stroking up): the chin goes up and up, purring, then it rubs its cheek on your knuckles (scent-marking you as its own) |
     | 蘇格蘭折耳貓 | 反手撓下巴: the head tilts further and further until the whole cat slowly topples sideways, melted |
     | 貓頭鷹 | under the face disc: face feathers fluffed out, eyes half shut, the head following your finger round and round until it is nearly upside down |
     | 小雞 | under the beak: head up, eyes shut, puffed into a round ball of fluff, 「啾～」 |
     | 小鴨 | under the bill: nibbles your finger with its bill, its bottom waggling |
     | 九尾 (beast) | 「哼」 while pressing her head into your hand, the nine tails swaying slowly; stop and a tail hooks your hand to keep it there |

   - **The Shiba's move is after 安西教練** (灌籃高手). That stays a reference only: no name from that work in the UI, the lines or a store page; in code `jowl_jiggle` or similar.
   - **The fox's human form** (Roger, 2026-11-25: yes): head (a pat; 傲嬌 「……只准一下」), hands (握手), shoulders (環抱, which she turns into her bear hug of you), chin (she catches your finger: 「挑姊姊下巴？膽子不小。」). Belly, legs and back get no dent and no action: she steps aside or swats the hand away. Zones on the body of a grown woman read differently from an animal's, and Steam's content survey asks. **Asked**: her cheeks and tails were her zones too (entry of 2026-11-25 above), and neither has that problem (her tails are her favourite spot); proposed to keep both. **Roger: yes** (item 6 gives both an action).
   - **The menu**: Roger said yes (2026-11-25) to a 「互動…」 submenu with 握手 and 舉高高, with 抱抱 kept. Missed when it was proposed: 抱抱… already has 環抱 / 舉高高 / 搖搖抱, and 叫牠… has 握手. **Proposed instead**: no new submenu (the menu was cut to nine rows in the twenty-sixth pass). **Roger: 可以**, no new submenu.

   **The zones built 2026-11-25** (`go on`; the picture `inbox/zones-shoulder-foot.png` waits for Roger's look):
   - **New zones** `Zone::Shoulder` and `Zone::Foot` (`ZONES` gains `shoulder` and `foot`). `Paw` is now a front paw only (the human form's hand). Made after the bake, when the whole pet is known (`mesh::limb_zones`, run on the baked skin and on meshes made elsewhere, so her figure is cut the same way):
     - **Front paw or foot**: skin in `paw` that moves mostly with a front leg's bones (`leg_f…`, `arm_…`, `fore_…`) stays a paw; the rest (hind legs, a bird's legs, her legs) is a foot. A four-legged pet's thigh counts too (skin more than 35% on a hind leg, `THIGH`): its hind paw alone was a few pixels, half under the rump.
     - **Shoulders, four legs**: the back and the chest's sides (facing sideways, normal x past 0.6) from 0.25 behind the front legs' joint forward, from that joint up: the withers walking side-on, the chest's upper edges under the head sitting up.
     - **Shoulders, birds**: the top 35% of each wing (`WING_ROOT`). Their body there is hidden under the head and the wings.
     - **Shoulders, the human form**: her robe within 0.16 of each arm's joint, from 0.06 below it up, and the top half of each upper arm. Her head is a chibi's, low over her shoulders: the robe alone left a line under her chin.
   - **What they do for now** (until the tap setting gives each its action): a shoulder is liked as the back is (`Species::fondness`), dents and tickles as the back does (a little smaller dent), and is not "behind": touched there it turns to face you. A foot is a paw in all of it (disliked, lifts when held).
   - **Hug hands** take the shoulders as body (`BODY_ZONES`), a bird's wing roots included (they stay close to its body).
   - **Not changed**: the sprite fallback (no GPU, `model.ron` through the raymarcher) still has `paw` for every foot and no shoulders, as it never had backs or wings.
   - **Checked**: `every_pet_has_shoulders_and_feet_apart_from_its_front_paws` (mutation-checked: fails with the pass off); `examples/zones.rs` now draws `fox-human` too, each pet at 100% and at 50% (the smallest the settings allow), and writes its legend; `examples/touch.rs` has `shoulder` and `foot`.
   - **Seen in the picture, for Roger**: sitting facing you, a four-legged pet's hind feet are under it and hardly show (walking side-on they do), so 舉高高 by its feet is a side-on thing; and the human form's chin covers her whole lower face, her cheeks small (for "the human form's zones", later in the order).

   **The tap setting built 2026-11-26** (Roger: 「那就開工吧」):
   - **「動作要點幾下」** (`Settings::taps`, 2 by default, kept to 1 or 2): in 互動與聲音 under 「摸摸牠們」, not in 一般 (an interaction setting, the same for every pet as asked), a `Segmented` 一下 / 兩下 with a hint for each.
   - **The double-click time** is the system's (`chonkgene_platform::double_click_time`: Windows `GetDoubleClickTime`, kept to 0.2–0.9 s; 0.4 s elsewhere). The old fixed 0.45 s and "a double-click anywhere is a hug" are gone.
   - **What a spot does** (`app::tap_action`): head 摸摸頭 (the menu's `pat_you`), front paw or wing 握手 (`shake_paw`: `Mind::shake_hands` gives 親密度 and a happy face, the paw given at once as an order, `Fun::ask_now`, saying 「好」 as 叫牠… › 握手 does; asleep or with a grudge it flinches and says so), hind paw 舉高高, shoulders 環抱, back 搖搖抱 (`hug_you` with that hold). Belly, cheeks, chin and tail have none yet. Her human form: head, hands and shoulders only.
   - **Two taps**: the first on a spot with an action is a small poke at once (`Animator::tap`, a third of a poke's kick, no hop, no look round); it looks round and reacts as to a poke once the double-click time has passed with no second tap (`Animator::noticed`, then `poke_now`). The spot is kept from the first tap (`Pet::tapped`). A tap where there is no action is a poke at once, no longer held back.
   - **One tap**: a quick tap on a spot with an action starts it; elsewhere a poke as now. Long presses are as they were in both modes.
   - **Tests**: `a_tap_does_what_the_spot_does` (app), `a_first_tap_waits_to_look_round` (anim, mutation-checked), `a_paw_is_given_gladly_unless_asleep_or_in_a_grudge` (mind), `taps_are_two_by_default_and_one_can_be_picked` (settings). Windows clippy of the platform crate clean.
   - **6 (b), the bottom pats, built the same day** as the bottom's action (`TapAction::PatBottom`; not on her human form): `Mind::pat_bottom` (line `bottom_pat`, all seven in three languages; 親密度 by how it likes its bottom, the Shiba most), `Species::bottom_pat` for how:
     - **Cats** `Act::ButtUp`: the body tipped forward 14°, the front legs kept straight, the tail turned straight up from wherever it rests (`Tail::raise`, worked out from its base-to-tip line, so the Fold's tail held out behind and the Munchkin's curled one both stand), the hind paws treading; sitting, it gets up for it.
     - **Shiba**: `Animator::pat_rump`, 1.8 s of `Touch::rump`, a hind leg kicking (the scratch's kick) and a look back over its shoulder even though it loves it there, pleased.
     - **Chick**: a startled hop forward, away from the hand. **Duckling**: a hop on the spot, then off at a quicker waddle (pace 1.9, 1.6 s), its 「呱！呱呱呱！」.
     - **Owl** (ours; Roger's list left it out): it looks round at the hand behind it, unimpressed, 「咕？（整顆頭轉過來盯著你）」.
     - **Fox** (beast form): she turns to the hand and swings her tails at it (`Act::Swish`), 「放肆！」. **No face for the glare yet**: 厭世 (FedUp) would melt her flat, so her face stays as it is until the faces of item 5 give her one.
     - **Tests**: `a_pat_on_the_bottom_is_taken_each_its_own_way` (mind), `patted_on_the_bottom_a_cat_puts_its_rump_up_and_the_shiba_kicks` (mesh). `examples/touch.rs` scenes `buttup`, `rump`, `swish`. Picture: `inbox/bottom-pats.png`.
   **「ㄉㄨㄞ ㄉㄨㄞ」 and 6 (a), the cheek squeeze, built 2026-11-26** (Roger: 「go on」, and 「跟毛色」 as the default hair colour: 可以, item 2 of the twenty-eighth pass):
   - **「ㄉㄨㄞ ㄉㄨㄞ」**: let go, a dent (a finger's, your hands' in a hug or on its cheeks) springs back on a looser spring than the one pressing (`mesh_frames::jiggle`: 4.6 Hz, damping 0.17 lean; 3.4 Hz, 0.09 the chubbiest), so the flesh bulges out past flat and wobbles: two or three bulges on a lean or an ordinary pet, four on the chubbiest. A hug let go wobbles the same way. **Not done**: the Shiba's double chin as a soft spot of its own (with its chin scratch, next).
   - **The squeeze**: a finger held still on a cheek for 0.6 s (`HOLD_SECS`, where it would have become a pat) brings your two hands, coming in over 0.3 s (`Touch::squeeze`; not asleep, not while the finger rubs about, which is a scratch). They are drawn as the hug's hands (`props::cheek_hands`, grip `Palm`: the heels on the outer edge of each cheek, the fingers up the sides of its head, leaning in) and press both cheeks toward each other (`CHEEK_DENT` 0.075, radius 0.24).
   - **The face squashes**: the head bone (and all on it) is squeezed along its own axes (`Skeleton::pose_with`): 16% narrower, 7% taller, the muzzle, bill or face disc pushed out 12%. Eyes > <, the mouth a pout (`Look::Pout`).
   - **Let go**: the head springs back out past round, wider and shorter, and wobbles with the cheeks (the same spring). Its cheeks stay **rosy** (`Touch::rosy`, deepest let go, fading over 5 s; half that while still squeezed): the blush 45% wider and a soft pink over it (`style.z` in paint.wgsl, never a red), and puffed out a little (`CHEEK_PUFF`) while rosy.
   - **What it does then** (`Mind::squeeze_cheeks`, line `cheek_squeeze`, all seven in three languages; 親密度 12/8/4 by how it likes its cheeks): a cheek it loves (曼赤肯, 貓頭鷹): pleased, it rubs its face on your hand, side to side (`Act::Nuzzle`). The rest: pouting, `Act::CheekRub`: sitting, the front paws up in front of it and the face rubbed into them (a chibi's legs cannot reach its cheeks, so the head comes down, as a cat washing does), a bird's wings up at its face, her human form holding her cheeks. The fox: 「捏、捏夠了沒！」. Asleep or with a grudge, as a pat.
   - **Not built**: the dislike after 3 s (no species dislikes its cheeks now). The sprite fallback has no hands or rosy cheeks, only the pout.
   - **Seen in the picture** (`inbox/cheek-squeeze.png`, every pet and her human form): the chick's and the duckling's wings rubbing read more like a flustered flap than a rub.
   - **Tests**: `a_finger_held_on_a_cheek_squeezes_it_rosy` (anim), `squeezed_cheeks_please_those_who_love_it_there` (mind), `let_go_flesh_wobbles_more_the_chubbier` (mesh_frames), `pressed_flesh_springs_back_when_let_go` now counts two bulges, `squeezed_cheeks_narrow_the_face_and_spring_back_out` (mesh: every form against itself left alone; mutation-checked). `examples/touch.rs` scene `squeeze`.
   - **Next in the order**: the chin scratches.
3. **「好朋友／主人」 shown untranslated** (asked where on 2026-11-25): Roger: 似乎解決, he will say if he sees it again. Closed.
4. **Roger asked: should each pet's interactions show in 「我的寵物」?** Proposed: yes, as a card **「摸摸筆記」** beside the pet's picture:
   - **Shown from the start**: the shared zone → action rows (頭 摸摸頭, 手 握手, 腳 舉高高, 肩 環抱, 背 搖搖抱). Otherwise nobody finds 舉高高 on the feet.
   - **Filled in by trying, on that pet**: what is its own: its chin scratch, its fondness hearts per zone (❤️❤️ / ❤️ / · / ✗), its specials (the 曼赤肯's trap, the Shiba's kick, the owl's head turn). Until then 「還沒試過」. A small store of what each pet has shown (zone and reaction) in its save; it belongs with the growth diary (Phase 5).
   - **Hovering a row** lights that zone on the preview (the zone colours of `examples/zones.rs`).
   - The tap setting of item 2 lives in 一般, not here: it is the same for every pet.
5. **Roger, the same day: 「❤️❤️ 愛心太多是不是會讓畫面太亂，用顏色淺淡來表示會不會比較好？觸發到一定程度是不是可以出粉紅泡泡，寵物臉上的開心表情更明顯 或 high到不行」.** Proposed:
   - **No hearts in 摸摸筆記** (replaces the hearts in item 4). The preview's zones are tinted by fondness: 最愛 deep pink, 喜歡 pale pink, 還好 no tint, 不喜歡 pale grey-blue. Dislike gets a hue of its own because a paler pink would read as "likes a little". Each row has a word in the same colour (最愛 / 喜歡 / 還好 / 不喜歡). A zone not tried yet has no tint, only a dotted outline. Deep and pale pink differ in lightness, the grey-blue in hue, so it reads without colour vision too.
   - **Happiness that builds while you pet** (`Mind`, built like the tickle: it climbs while a hand is on it and fades with a half-life when it stops; fastest on a loved spot, slower on a liked one; a spot it does not mind stops at stage 1; a disliked one never starts):

     | Stage | Reached | Face | Body | Effect |
     |---|---|---|---|---|
     | 1 舒服 | about 1 s | eyes narrowed, the mouth's ends up, a faint blush | leans into the hand | now and then one heart |
     | 2 超開心 | about 3 s on a loved spot, 6 s on a liked one | eyes curved into ^ ^, mouth open, the blush deeper and wider | louder purr or pant, a cat treading, the tail going faster | pink bubbles rising slowly, now and then one pops |
     | 3 high到不行 | a loved spot only, about 3 s more | the species' own (below) | the species' own | a gush of bubbles, a pink glow round it |

   - **Stage 3, by species**: 柴犬, tongue out, eyes shut happy, tail spinning like a propeller, and let go it does zoomies (laps at full tilt); 曼赤肯, rolls over kneading the air, purring at its loudest (its belly still ends in the trap); 折耳貓, melts flat into a puddle, eyes thin lines, ears flat with bliss; 貓頭鷹, the head turns a full circle, face feathers puffed to twice their size, eyes crescents; 小雞, puffed round and bouncing like a ball, 「啾啾啾」; 小鴨, spins on the spot, tail waggling madly, a happy quack; 九尾, 傲嬌 gives way: face red, paws (hands, in human form) over her face, 「才、才沒有很舒服！」 while all nine tails wag wildly. A 厭世 one keeps a straight face while its bubbles give it away.
   - **After stage 3**: a few seconds of afterglow (the bubbles thinning), then a cooldown of 1–2 min in which it climbs no higher than stage 2, so the peak stays special. 親密度 for the peak once, within the daily cap.
   - **Fewer hearts on the desktop too**: hearts belong to stage 1 only (now one every 1.6 s while held on a loved spot); stage 2 has bubbles instead.
   - Pets are small on the desktop, so the face is pushed further than a still would need: the blush (`face.rs`, the `face.blush` spot) grows in size and strength with the stage. The bubbles are a new `Fx` (not `Fx::Bubble`, which is the spell's).
6. **Roger, the same day**: (a) 「你雙手夾臉頰肉 放開後再回彈後 兩邊臉頰就會有紅腫」, (b) 「對屁股方面的互動需要嗎？」, (c) 「摸九尾的尾巴她會害羞？」. Proposed:
   - **(a) Cheek squeeze**, every pet and the fox's human form:
     - **Trigger**: a long press on a cheek (the "cheek squish" §1.2 always had). It takes the place of a pat there.
     - **Held**: your two hands come in from both sides, drawn as the hug's (`props::hug_arms`), and press both cheeks in with the shader's two presses. The face squashes and the mouth is pushed into a pout: a duck's bill pushed forward, the owl's face disc squeezed into an oval. It stays squashed while held.
     - **Let go**: the cheeks spring out past round and wobble (「ㄉㄨㄞ ㄉㄨㄞ」), then stay **rosy** for about 5 s: the blush deeper, wider and a little puffed, fading. Pink, never a sore red-purple: cute, not hurt.
     - **By fondness**: on a loved cheek it is pleased and rubs its face on your hand after. On one it does not mind or dislikes, it pouts and rubs its cheeks with its paws. Held over about 3 s on a disliked cheek, it wriggles out (a dislike, as now). The fox holds her cheeks, red: 「捏、捏夠了沒！」.
   - **(b) The bottom: a light pat** (two taps, or one in that mode), species-true:
     - **Cats**: elevator butt at the tail base (rump up, tail straight up, treading).
     - **Shiba**: a hind leg kicking at the tail base, and a pleased look back.
     - **Chick and duckling**: a startled hop one step forward; the duckling waddles off faster, quacking.
     - **Beast-form fox**: she turns, glares and swats your hand with her tails, 「放肆！」. A glare, no blush, not shyness.
     - **Unchanged**: her human form has no bottom zone (item 2). Pokes and long presses on the bottom stay as they are.
   - **(c) The fox's tails make her shy**, in both forms. Still her favourite spot (❤️❤️), so item 5's stages run as on any loved spot, but her face says the opposite of her tails:
     - **Stage 1**: a blush, a look away, 「尾、尾巴不准隨便摸……」, while the stroked tail curls round your hand.
     - **Stage 2**: red to the ears, paws over her face, tails fluffing up.
     - **Stage 3**: all nine fluff up to twice their size, 「笨、笨蛋！」, and she hides behind them, peeking out. Item 5's peak (「才、才沒有很舒服！」) stays for her other loved spots.
     - **Tone**: the lines stay 傲嬌 embarrassed, never suggestive (Steam's content survey, item 2).

**Suggested order**: 1 (small, on its own) → the zones (shoulder, front and hind paws) and their picture → the tap setting, mapped onto the actions already built, with 6 (b) → 「ㄉㄨㄞ ㄉㄨㄞ」 and 6 (a) → the chin scratches → 5's stages and bubbles, with 6 (c) → 摸肚肚 and 翻肚討摸 → the human form's zones → the cursor poses → 4 (摸摸筆記, coloured as 5 says).

### Roger's twenty-eighth pass (ideas, 2026-11-26; proposals, his answers owed)

Roger: 「你覺得這個主意如何」, four ideas for the fox, with `inbox/full.jpeg` (a face from an anime, for the gravity spell: a reference for the look only, as the Shiba's 安西教練 was; nothing from that work in the app, the lines or a store page). Picture of today's colours: `inbox/fox-colours-now.png`.

1. **A hairdo 「中分長髮」 so her seal shows.** Yes: today every hairdo has a fringe, and the casting crest (lotus grown over the forehead) hides under it. A fourth style in `figure_blend.py`, as the other three (each builds its own fringe in `fringe_and_locks`): parted in the middle, curtain locks framing the face, long straight hair down her back, no tie. 髮型 gains it. Medium (Blender, meshbake, the three hairdos' review loop).
2. **Gold and night: her hair and her tails do not match.** Seen: the 金色 hair (#EFA04C, tips #FFE3B5) is lighter and yellower than the gold tails (#F0A050 to white tips), and the 夜色 hair (#4B4262, tips #8A96D6) bluer than the night tails (#4A4060 to cyan tips #9FE3FF); hair is also glossier than fur, so it reads lighter. **Proposed**: 金色 and 夜色 hair take the same-named coat's tail colours (hair = `fur`, tips = `tailtip`, through `tailmid`), so the same name matches; and a 髮色 choice 「跟毛色」 that follows the coat when it changes. **Asked**: whether 「跟毛色」 becomes the default (Roger set white as the default on 2026-11-16). **Roger: 可以**, 「跟毛色」 is the default.
3. **One colour per coat for her seal, her casting glow (護體金光) and her magic**: 白色 red, 雪狐 pale blue, 金色 gold, 夜色 dark purple. Yes; today the seal follows the coat but the effects do not: the fox-fire and the spell's bubble are always blue and pink (`props::spell`, `bubble`), and her tails always burn white, gold, orange to red (`mesh.wgsl` `flame_tails`). **Proposed**: a coat colour `magic` (beast and human form) that the seal, the floor ring, the column of light, the fox-fire, the bubble and the burning tails all take (the flames white at the root through the coat's colour to a deeper tip). Facts and caveats told to Roger:
   - Today 金色's seal is red (#E8454F) and 夜色's pale blue (#7FD4FF), not gold and dark purple, so those two change.
   - A gold seal on the gold beast's orange fur, and a dark purple one on the night beast's dark purple fur, hardly show. **Proposed**: gold with a thin dark amber edge; for night a bright violet seal (same on both forms), and its magic dark violet mist round a bright lilac core, since a dark colour cannot glow (it reads as a shadow).
4. **New spells: 天雷 and 重力法術.** Both fit her (定身術, 祝福術, 招喚術 and 變身 are there; 施法… gains two rows).
   - **天雷**: a bolt from above, a white flash on the pet, and its fur stands on end (the shell's fuzz longer and spiky, the body a little puffed; a bird's feathers as `PuffUp`), a wisp of smoke from its head, eyes spinning; it shakes itself like a wet dog (the shake is there) and its fur settles. **Proposed**: no blackened soot (cute, not hurt; Steam's content survey). The zapped one sulks at her for a while or, in 非理性模式, chases her.
   - **重力法術**: every pet it hits is pressed flat, its face pulled into a squashed, scrunched grimace (eyes narrowed sideways, wrinkles drawn in ink, mouth open, cheeks sagging, a duck's bill and the owl's disc squashed); let go, it springs up past its height and wobbles back (「ㄉㄨㄞ ㄉㄨㄞ」, the press dent's overshoot planned in item 2 of the twenty-seventh pass), with an impact: a flash ring, speed lines, the pet's own window shaking. **Proposed**: never the whole screen shaking or flashing (§1.7, don't disturb).
   - **Asked**: who they hit (proposed: 天雷 one pet, the one squabbling with her or the nearest; 重力 everyone round her, as 定身術), and whether she casts them by herself (proposed: only in 非理性模式, rarely; from 施法… any time).

5. **Roger, the same day: colours of her own choosing** for her hair, tails, seal, spells (法術) and casting glow (護體魔力), the preset colours still there to pick while customising. Yes; it is item 3's `magic` colour and item 2's hair made open. **Proposed**:
   - **Each row** in her settings card (髮色, 尾巴, 咒印, 法術, 護體魔力): the preset dots as now (「跟毛色」 first, then each coat's or hair's colours), and last a 「自訂」 dot. Tapped, it opens our own cute picker under the row (no stock colour dialog): about twenty soft swatches in the UI's style, a hue strip and a lightness strip. Picking a preset dot again leaves the custom colour remembered in the 「自訂」 dot, to go back to; each row has 「恢復預設」.
   - **One colour a row**: the tips worked out from it (hair tips and tail tips lighter, as the presets' two-tone ones); 紅髮's red to gold stays a preset.
   - **Kept looking right whatever is picked**: a seal too close to the fur or skin under it gets a thin edge, dark or light; a dark glow is drawn as mist of that colour round a lightened core, since a dark colour cannot glow. On the beast form the tails' root stays her body's fur and the picked colour runs from the middle to the tips.
   - **Where it shows at once**: the seal, spells and glow are shader and effect colours, live while dragging. Hair and tails are baked into her mesh, so they show when the strip is let go, as a 髮色 change remakes her now (in fox-fire).
   - **Per pet**, as coat and hair are (`PetSave`), and for both forms.
   - **Asked**: 法術 = what lands on others (定身術's bubble, 天雷's bolt, 重力's press, 祝福術's hearts) and 護體魔力 = the light round her while casting (floor ring, column, fox-fire, burning tails); one colour or two for hair and tails (proposed one, tips worked out); whether the other pets get 「自訂」 coat colours later.
   - Built after 3 and 2, on their colour plumbing.

**Order, proposed**: after the twenty-seventh pass's tap setting: 3 and 2 (small, colour data and the effects reading it) → 5 (the picker on top of them) → 1 (Blender) → 4 (new effects, the face and fur work, lines in three languages). Roger's call.

**Roger, 2026-11-26: 「那就開工吧」**, read as the proposals above taken as they stand (法術 and 護體魔力 split as proposed, one colour a row with tips worked out, the other pets' own colours not now; 天雷 one pet and 重力 everyone round her, cast by herself only in 非理性模式) and the order with them, after the twenty-seventh pass's tap setting and bottom pats. Asked without a proposal, so still his to say: whether 「跟毛色」 becomes the default hair colour (white until then, his rule of 2026-11-16). **Roger, 2026-11-26: 可以**: 「跟毛色」 becomes the default hair colour when item 2 is built (white until then).

### New species: eleven more pets (Roger, 2026-11-13; planned, none built)

Roger, 2026-11-13: 「這是 chonkGene 接下來要新增的寵物」, with a concept picture he made with Gemini (`inbox/Gemini_Generated_Image_1w2xeh1w2xeh1w2x.jpeg`, 「超萌動物大集合」; a reference for look only, nothing from it ships). It shows nine animals; the Munchkin is already in the cast, so eight are new. Roger then added three not in the picture (same day: 「再加個 無尾熊 和 熊貓 台灣黑熊」): the koala, the giant panda and the Formosan black bear, eleven in all. When they come relative to the open items above is Roger's call (his order of 2026-11-13 is to clear what is planned first).

Defaults in the form of §1.1 (personality rolls near them on adoption). Names follow the cast's food names.

| Species | Default name | Ticklish | Patience | Energy | Daily rhythm | Signature moves |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Guinea pig (天竺鼠) | 布丁 (tricolour, like caramel pudding) | medium | low (startles easily) | medium | daytime, many short naps | **popcorning** (hops straight up, twisting, when happy); **wheeking** 「噗依噗依！」 when it hears you or wants food; freezes stock-still when startled; never stops chewing; scurries along a window's bottom edge |
| Netherland dwarf rabbit (侏儒兔) | 麻糬 | low (dislikes being held) | low | high at dawn and dusk | **crepuscular**: lively early and late, dozes midday | **binky** (leaps, twisting in the air); **the flop** (tips over on its side when content); thumps a hind foot when annoyed; nose always twitching; washes its face with its front paws; moves by **hopping**, not walking |
| Red panda (小貓熊) | 楓糖 | medium | medium | low | **crepuscular**, sleeps much of the day | **the threat pose** (stands up on its hind legs, arms up: fierce and absurdly cute) when poked too often; sleeps draped over a window's top edge, legs hanging; curls its ringed tail round its face to sleep; licks its paws |
| Capybara (水豚) | 柚子 (the yuzu on its head) | low | **very high** (the calmest of all) | low | daytime | **a yuzu on its head** in the pond (onsen), eyes half shut; **others sit on it**: the birds and small ones climb onto its back and it does not mind; never squabbles in irrational mode, sits through it unmoved; long contented 「嗯——」 |
| Sea otter (海獺) | 貝果 | high | medium | high | daytime | **floats on its back** in the pond; cracks a shell on its belly with a stone; keeps a favourite stone tucked under its arm; **holds paws** with another floating pet so neither drifts off asleep; rubs its face with both paws; on land, a humped, bounding lope |
| Long-tailed tit, Hokkaido (銀喉長尾山雀) | 大福 | medium | low | high | **early riser** like the chick | tiny round white puff on a long tail; hops; hangs upside down from a window's edge; **huddles in a row** with others (several, or any small pets) on a ledge, pushing to the middle; 「啾哩哩」 |
| Emperor penguin chick (帝王企鵝幼崽, grey-fluff stage) | 芝麻 | medium | high | medium | daytime | waddles, flippers out for balance; **tobogganing** (slides on its belly, §8 eighteenth pass, as its everyday way to go fast); falls over and cannot get up quickly; **huddles** with the others when it is cold or late; tufts of grey fluff come loose |
| Sand cat (沙漠貓) | 沙琪瑪 | low | low | high at night | **nocturnal** (as the owl) | digs in the beach's sand and naps in the hole; ears flatten wide and low; a **bark-like call** (sand cats really do), startling from something so small; looks fierce, is tiny; walks with soft, padded big feet |
| Koala (無尾熊) | 芋泥 (its lavender grey) | low | medium | **very low** | sleeps most of the day (real koalas sleep 18–20 h), awake a little in the evening | **clings** to the side of a window as to a tree trunk, arms and legs round it, and dozes there; chews a eucalyptus leaf very slowly; clings to another pet the same way (the capybara most of all, which does not mind); a surprisingly deep, loud bellow for its size |
| Giant panda (熊貓) | 竹筍 | high | high | low | daytime, long naps after eating | **sits with its legs splayed out** eating bamboo, held in both paws; tumbles: rolls over clumsily, somersaults, slides off a window's edge and lands in a heap unbothered; lies on its back, paws in the air; bleats softly (pandas do, not a roar) |
| Formosan black bear (台灣黑熊) | 黑糖 | medium | medium | high | daytime; eats much more in autumn (the system clock's season, as real ones fatten on acorns) | the **white V on its chest** (月牙): **stands up on its hind legs** to look about and sniff, showing it; climbs to a window's top edge; swims and swats at the pond's water as if after a fish; a huffing snort and jaw pop when cross |

How each is built, by what is nearest (`assets/species/<id>/model.ron` plus `quad.ron` or `biped.ron`, `Species`, `Build` in `props.rs` for the hands of the hugs):

- **Sand cat**: a cat (`Build::Cat { short: false }`), new coat (sandy, faint stripes on the legs), wide low ears, big paws. The cheapest; a good first.
- **Red panda**: four-legged, the dog's or cat's skeleton with a long thick ringed tail (a tail chain like the Shiba's, longer); its threat pose is a new pose on the hind legs (`Posture`), the way the owl's head turn was its own.
- **Guinea pig, capybara**: a new low build, a loaf with short legs nearly hidden and no tail (`Build::Rodent`, hands: cupped from below, as birds, for the guinea pig; the capybara is held round the middle). The capybara is the same shape at a bigger size and with a blunt square muzzle.
- **Rabbit**: four-legged with long hind legs; a **hop** gait (both hind legs together, front paws touching down between) in place of the walk; long ears as bones that swing.
- **Sea otter**: four-legged on land; a floating-on-its-back pose for the pond (the cradle's roll-over, §8 twenty-first pass item 2, held in the water), paws on its chest. Its home is the pond: it goes there more than anyone.
- **Long-tailed tit**: a bird (`Build::Bird`), the chick's biped with a long two-feather tail; smaller than any pet so far.
- **Penguin chick**: a bird, upright, flippers in place of wings (they flap but do not fly: it hangs under balloons like the Shiba when it flies); the belly slide is already there.
- **Panda, Formosan black bear**: a new bear build (`Build::Bear`: round and heavy, short legs, a stub tail, round ears; held round the middle like the capybara, too heavy to lift by the armpits), the dog's skeleton widened. The panda sits splayed (a new sitting pose); the black bear's standing up is the red panda's pose on its hind legs at a bigger size, so the two share it.
- **Koala**: the bear build's skeleton with a big round nose, fluffy round ears and no tail; its clinging pose is new (arms and legs round the upright side of a window, as the owl's `Scene::Perch` sits on a top edge).

Each gets its own lines in `assets/lines/zh-TW.ron` (§1.11), sounds, `fondness` by zone (Roger's table), `habits`, `plays_like`, `Grumpiness` (guinea pig: freezes; rabbit: thumps; red panda: the threat pose; capybara: does not wake grumpy; otter: rolls over and away; tit and penguin: `PuffUp`; sand cat: `Swat`; koala: does not wake, only grumbles; panda: rolls over, back to sleep; black bear: the huffing snort), and a place on the adoption screen.

Decisions (all settled; build after the queued pass items above):
1. ~~How many on the desktop by default~~ **Settled (Roger, 2026-11-13: 「我覺得桌面7隻就夠了」)**: the default count stays 7 (§1.1), no longer "every species once" now that there are 18. The new ones rest in the pet house until swapped in; the adoption screen lets one pick any. Which seven come out by default on first run is the original cast unless the adoption screen picks others.
2. **Build order, settled (Roger, 2026-11-13: 「都先加進 plan」)**: sand cat → red panda → penguin chick → long-tailed tit (all near a build that exists), then guinea pig and capybara (the new low build together), then panda and Formosan black bear (the bear build together), koala (on the bear build, plus the cling), then rabbit (the hop) and sea otter (floating).
3. **Names, settled the same way**: the default names in the tables above (布丁, 麻糬, 楓糖, 柚子, 貝果, 大福, 芝麻, 沙琪瑪, 芋泥, 竹筍, 黑糖); Roger can rename any later, and users rename their own in settings as now.

### Phase 1: Shiba prototype (feel) — `v0.1.0`

- The art pipeline (§3.3) works end to end for one Shiba, with placeholder art.
- Procedural animation: breathing, blinking, tail, belly spring.
- Poking: five hit zones, three tickle stages, both the laughing and fed-up routes.
- Lift, throw, land; walk along the bottom of the work area.
- Sound switch, minimal right-click menu, single instance.

**Done when**: Roger has poked it on a real machine and confirms the feel is right; golden PNGs exist; one pet meets the §5 budget.

**Status (2026-10-22, branch `feat/phase1-shiba`)**
- Done: art pipeline with standard/mochi morph targets and four coats; procedural breathing, blinking, tail and belly spring; five hit zones; three tickle stages with the laugh and fed-up routes; lift, shake, throw, landing squash; walking along the work area; golden PNGs; the §5 budget at 100% on the development machine. CI builds the app for all three targets.
- The laugh, fed-up and overdone reactions are **procedural placeholders** (squint and giggle bounce, half-lidded eyes, eyes shut): the real belly-up, playing-dead and turned-away poses need keyframed clips and art (§10).
- Still to do: sound switch, minimal right-click menu, single instance.

### Phase 2: behaviour, sleep and don't disturb — `v0.2.0`

- Full behaviour system (§1.3): action library, needs, daily rhythm, idle, cursor curiosity.
- Sleep and grumpiness (§1.12): snot bubble, sleep talk, the three ways of waking, Shiba zoomies, grudges and calming down.
- Core of mood and affinity (§1.13): only goes up, daily cap; level and state in the right-click menu; rename and 「牠怎麼叫你」.
- All don't-disturb mechanisms (§1.7, §3.5); Linux mic detection is decided here.
- Tray, global hotkey, saved settings, portable mode, start at login, menu entries, remove all traces.

**Done when**: the §7 behaviour tests pass; the "don't disturb" part of the manual checklist passes in all four environments.

### Phase 3: the whole cast and irrational mode — `v0.3.0`

- Art and rigs for Munchkin, Fold, Owl (270° head turn, puffing up), Chick (feather burst, pecking) and Duckling (waddle, follow-the-leader).
- Individual personalities, relationship table, 7 skits (including 雞同鴨講 and the duckling parade), breaking it up.
